...in *Book II*. And when they are clean and pure, let each put on garments of white linen, pure and clean, using the prayers and ceremonies described in *Book II*. After this, let the *Master* alone say the confession. When this is finished, the *Master*, as a sign of penitence, will kiss <note>original: "Kiss"; this refers to the "holy kiss" mentioned in the New Testament. See footnote below.</note> the *Disciples* on the forehead, and each of them will kiss the other. Afterward, let the Master extend his hands over the *Disciples*, and as a sign of absolution, absolve and bless them. When this is done, he will distribute to each of his Disciples the instruments necessary for the Magical Art, which they are to carry into the Circle.

The *First Disciple* will carry the *Censer* <note>An incense burner.</note>, the *Perfumes*, and the *Temple Incense*; <note>Temple Incense and Special Waxen Candles can be obtained from Messrs. de Laurence, Scott & Co. by consulting their Catalogue. <term>Note:</term> This note was added by 20th-century occult publishers who sold ritual supplies.</note> the *Second Disciple* will carry the *Book*, *Papers*, *Pens*, *Ink*, and any foul-smelling or impure materials; the *Third* will carry the *Knife* and the *Sickle* of *Magical Art*, the *Lantern*, and the *Candles*; the *Fourth* will carry the *Psalms* and the rest of the *Instruments*; the *Fifth* will carry the *Crucible* or *Chafing-dish* <note>A portable stove or container for hot coals.</note>, and the *Charcoal* or *Fuel*. However, it is necessary for the Master himself to carry the *Staff* and the *Wand* or *Rod* in his hand. Once the necessary things are arranged in this way, the Master will go with his Disciples to the designated place where they intend to construct the Circle for the Magical Arts and experiments, repeating the prayers and orations found in *Book II* along the way.

When the *Master* has arrived at the appointed place with his *Disciples*, he—having lit the flame of the fire and having exorcised <note>Spiritually purified through a specific prayer or command.</note> it again as laid down in the *Second Book*—shall light the Candle and place it in the Lantern. One of the Disciples must hold this in his hand at all times to light the *Master* at his work. Now, whenever the Master of the Art needs to speak with the Spirits for some specific purpose, he must try to form certain Circles that differ slightly and have a specific connection to the particular experiment being performed. To succeed in forming such a Circle for the Magical Art with greater certainty and effectiveness, you shall construct it in the following manner:

### THE CONSTRUCTION OF THE CIRCLE.

Take the Knife, the Sickle, or the Sword of Magical Art, consecrated according to the manner and order that we shall provide for you in the Second Book. With this Knife or Sickle of Art, you shall draw a second circle beyond the inner Circle <note>original: "circumferential line"; i.e., within the first circle.</note> which you have already formed, surrounding the other at a distance of one foot and having the same center. <note>original: "i.e. Two Circles enclosed between three circumferential lines."</note> Within this one-foot space between the first and the second boundary lines, you shall trace toward the Four Quarters of the Earth <note>i.e., the four Cardinal points of the compass: North, South, East, and West.</note> the Sacred and Venerable Symbols...

...symbols of the holy letter Tau <note>original: "Tau"; the 22nd and final letter of the Hebrew alphabet, historically associated with the mark of protection or the shape of a cross.</note>.* Between the first and second circles†—which you shall have drawn yourself with the instrument of *magical art*—you must make four six-sided pentacles <note>original: "hexagonal pentacles"; while "pentacle" often implies five points, here the text specifies a six-pointed shape or hexagram.</note>,‡ and between these you shall write four awe-inspiring and powerful names of God, as follows:

Between the East and the South, the supreme name IHVH, the *Tetragrammaton* <note>original: "Tetragrammaton"; the four-letter name of God, often transliterated as Yahweh or Jehovah.</note>;

Between the South and the West, the essential *four-letter* name AHIH, Eheieh <note>original: "Eheieh"; Hebrew for "I Will Be," the name God gave to Moses at the burning bush.</note>;

Between the West and the North, the name of power ALIVN, Elion <note>original: "Elion"; Hebrew for "The Most High."</note>;

And between the North and the East, the great name ALH, Eloah <note>original: "Eloah"; a singular form of the Hebrew word for God.</note>;

These names are of supreme importance in the list of the Sephiroth,§ and their ruling counterparts.

Furthermore, you shall draw two squares around these circles, with their corners turned toward the four cardinal directions <note>original: "Four Quarters of the Earth"; East, West, South, and North.</note>; the space between the lines of the outer and inner square shall be half a foot. The outermost corners of the outer square shall be used as the centers of four smaller circles, each measuring one foot in diameter. All of these are to be drawn with the knife or the consecrated instrument of art. Within these four circles, you must write these four names of the most holy God in this order:

At the East: AL, El;
At the West: IH, Yah;
At the South: AGLA, Agla <note>A Qabalistic acronym for "Ata Gibor Leolam Adonai," meaning "You, O Lord, are mighty forever."</note>;
And at the North: ADNI, Adonai <note>Hebrew for "Lord."</note>.

Between the two squares, the name *Tetragrammaton* is to be written in the same way as is shown in the illustration. (*See Figure 2.*)

While constructing the circle, the Master should recite the following Psalms: Psalm 2, Psalm 54, Psalm 113, Psalm 67, Psalm 47, and Psalm 68.

Alternatively, he may recite them before he begins tracing the circle.

When this is finished, and the incense rituals <note>original: "fumigations"; the burning of aromatic herbs or resins to purify the area.</note> have been performed as described in the chapter on incense in the Second Book, the Master should gather his disciples together. He should encourage, reassure, and strengthen them, then lead them into the sections of the magic circle. He must place them at the four cardinal directions, exhorting them to fear nothing and to stay in the positions assigned to them. Additionally, the disciple placed toward the East should have a pen, ink, paper, silk,

...and white cotton, all clean and suitable for the work. Furthermore, each of the companions should have a new sword drawn in his hand (in addition to the consecrated <term>Magical Sword of Art</term> <note>The primary ritual blade used by the master to command spirits.</note>), and he should keep his hand resting upon its hilt. He should not leave his assigned place or move from it under any pretext.

After this, the master should leave the circle, light the fuel in the earthen pots, and place the censers <note>vessels used for burning incense</note> upon them at the four cardinal directions <note>original: "Four Quarters of the Earth"</note>. He should have the consecrated wax candle in his hand, light it, and place it in a hidden, secret spot prepared for it. After this, let him re-enter and close the circle.

The master should again encourage his disciples and explain everything they must do and observe. They should then promise and vow to carry out these commands.

*The master should then repeat this prayer:*

#### PRAYER.

> As we enter here with all humility, let God the Almighty enter into this circle through an entrance of eternal happiness, divine prosperity, perfect joy, abundant charity, and eternal greeting. Let all the demons fly from this place, especially those who oppose this work, and let the angels of peace assist and protect this circle, from which let all discord and strife depart. Magnify and extend your most holy name upon us, O Lord, and bless our conversation and our assembly. Sanctify our humble entry here, O Lord our God, the blessed and holy one of the eternal ages! Amen.

*After this, let the master say the following while on his knees:*

#### PRAYER.

> O Lord God, all-powerful and all-merciful, you who do not desire the death of a sinner but rather that he may turn from his wickedness and live; give and grant your grace to us by blessing and consecrating this earth and this circle, which is marked out here with the most powerful and holy names of God. And I conjure you, O Earth, by the most holy name of <term>ASHER EHEIEH</term> <note>original: "Asher Eheieh"; Hebrew for "I am that I am," the name revealed to Moses in Exodus.</note> entering within this circle, which was composed and made by my hand. And may God, even <term>ADONAI</term> <note>original: "Adonai"; a Hebrew title for God meaning "Lord."</note>, bless this place with all the virtues of heaven, so that no obscene or unclean spirit may have the power to enter into this circle or to annoy any person within it; through the Lord God <term>ADONAI</term>, who lives eternally through the ages of ages. Amen.

I beseech you, O Lord God, the all-powerful and all-merciful, that you would deign to bless this circle, this entire place, and everyone who is within it. Grant to us, your servants who recount nothing but the wonders of your law, a good angel for our guardian. Remove every opposing power from us; preserve us from evil and from trouble; grant, O Lord, that we may rest in this place in all...

...safety, through you, O Lord, who lives and reigns forever and ever <note>original: "unto the Ages of the Ages," a translation of the Latin liturgical phrase *in saecula saeculorum*</note>. Amen.

Let the master now rise and place upon his head a crown made of paper (or any other appropriate material), on which must be written (using the colors and other necessary items which we shall describe later) these four names: **AGLA**, **AGLAI**, **AGLATA**, **AGLATAI** <note>These are variations of the powerful divine name AGLA, which is a Hebrew acronym for *Atah Gibor Le-olam Adonai*, meaning "You, O Lord, are mighty forever."</note>. These names are to be placed on the front, the back, and on either side of the head.

Furthermore, the master ought to have with him in the circle those <term>pentacles</term> <note>magical talismans or medals inscribed with sacred symbols or names of power</note> or medals which are necessary for his purpose. These are described later in this book and should be constructed according to the rules given in the chapter on *Pentacles*. They should be drawn on <term>virgin paper</term> <note>paper or parchment that has never been used for any other purpose, signifying spiritual purity</note> with a pen; the ink, blood, or colors must be prepared according to the methods we will show later in the chapters on those subjects. It will be sufficient to take only those pentacles which are actually required; they should be sewn to the front of the linen robe, over the chest, with the consecrated <term>Needle of Art</term> and with a thread that has been woven by a young girl.

After this, let the master turn toward the East (unless directed otherwise, or unless he wishes to call spirits which belong to another quarter of the universe) and recite the conjuration contained in this chapter with a loud voice. And if the spirits are disobedient and do not then appear, he must rise and take the exorcised <term>Knife of Art</term> <note>a ritual blade used to command spirits and direct energy</note> with which he constructed the circle, and raise it toward the sky as if he wished to beat or strike the air, and conjure the spirits. Let him then lay his right hand and the knife upon the pentacles or medals—constructed of and drawn upon virgin paper—which are fastened to or sewn upon his breast, and let him repeat the following conjuration while on his knees:

### CONJURATION.

> O Lord, hear my prayer, and let my cry come to you. O Lord God Almighty, who reigned before the beginning of time, and who by your infinite wisdom created the heavens, the earth, the sea, and all that is in them—all that is visible and all that is invisible—by a single word; I praise you, I bless you, I adore you, I glorify you, and I pray to you now at this time to be merciful to me, a miserable sinner, for I am the work of your hands. Save me and direct me by your holy name, you to whom nothing is difficult and nothing is impossible. Deliver me from the night of my ignorance and enable me to go forth from it. Enlighten me with a spark of your infinite wisdom. Take away from my senses the desire for greed and the wickedness of my idle words. Give to me, your servant, a wise understanding and a sharp, subtle heart to learn and understand all sciences and arts. Give me the capacity to hear and the strength of memory to retain them, so that I may be able to accomplish my desires, and under—

...understand and learn all difficult and desirable sciences; and also that I may be able to comprehend the hidden secrets of the Holy Scriptures. Give me the power to conceive them, so that I may be able to bring forth and speak my words with patience and humility, for the instruction of others, as You have commanded me.

> O God, the Father, All-Powerful and All-Merciful, Who has created all things, Who knows and understands them all, and to Whom nothing is hidden and nothing is impossible; I beg Your Grace for me and for Your servants. You see and know well that we do not perform this work to test Your Strength and Your Power as if in doubt, but rather so that we may know and understand the truth of all hidden things. I beg You to be kind and favorable toward us; by Your Splendor, Your Magnificence, and Your Holiness, and by Your Holy, Terrible, and Ineffable Name IAH <note>IAH: A shortened form of the Hebrew divine name Yahweh (Jehovah), often used in mystical contexts.</note>, at which the whole world trembles, and by the fear with which all creatures obey You. Grant, O Lord, that we may become responsive to Your Grace, so that through it we may have full confidence in and knowledge of You, and that the spirits may reveal themselves here in our presence. May those that are gentle and peaceable come to us, so that they may be obedient to Your commands, through You, O Most Holy Adonai <note>Adonai: A Hebrew title meaning "Lord," used as a substitute for the four-letter name of God.</note>, Whose Kingdom is an everlasting Kingdom, and Whose Empire lasts forever and ever <note>original: "unto the Ages of the Ages," a literal translation of the Latin liturgical phrase *in saecula saeculorum*</note>. Amen.

After saying all these words devoutly, let the Master rise and place his hands upon the <term>Pentacles</term> <note>Pentacles: Sacred talismans or medals inscribed with magical symbols and divine names used to protect the magician and control spirits.</note>. Let one of the companions hold the Book open before the Master, who, raising his eyes to heaven and turning toward the four quarters of the universe <note>the four cardinal directions: East, West, North, and South.</note>, shall say:

> O Lord, be for me a *Tower of Strength* against the appearance and assault of the evil spirits.

After this, turning toward the four quarters of the universe, he shall say the following words:

> These are the symbols and the names of the Creator which can bring terror and fear to you. Obey me then, by the power of these holy names, and by these mysterious symbols of the *Secret of Secrets*.

Once this is said and done, you will see them draw near and approach from all sides. But if they are hindered, detained, or busy in some way so that they cannot come, or if they are unwilling to come, then perform the <term>suffumigations</term> <note>the burning of incense or other aromatic substances as a ritual offering or to attract spirits</note> and censings again. After the disciples have again, by special order, touched their swords, and the Master has encouraged his disciples, he shall redraw the circle with the <term>Knife of Art</term> <note>Knife of Art: A consecrated magical tool used for ritual purposes, traditionally having a white handle to distinguish it from the black-handled *athame*.</note>. Raising the knife toward the sky, he shall strike the air with it. After this, he shall lay his hand upon the pentacles and, having knelt before the Most High, he shall humbly repeat the following confession. His disciples shall do the same, reciting it in a low and humble voice so they can scarcely be heard.*

---

* So as not to interfere with the direction of the Master's will <note>original: "Will-currents," a term from 19th-century occultism referring to the mental energy or focus directed by the practitioner.</note>.

# BOOK ONE
## CHAPTER IV
### THE CONFESSION TO BE MADE BY THE EXORCIST
#### CONFESSION

> O LORD of Heaven and Earth, before You I confess my sins and lament them, cast down and humbled in Your presence. For I have sinned before You through pride, greed <note>original: "avarice"</note>, and a boundless desire for honors and riches; by laziness, gluttony, greed, lustful excess <note>original: "debauchery"</note>, and drunkenness; because I have offended You with all kinds of sins of the flesh, adultery, and moral corruptions <note>original: "pollutions"</note>, which I have committed myself and allowed others to commit with my consent; by sacrilege, theft, robbery <note>original: "rapine"</note>, violation, and murder <note>original: "homicide"</note>; by the evil use I have made of my possessions, by my wastefulness <note>original: "prodigality"</note>, and by the sins I have committed against Hope and Charity. I have sinned by my bad advice, flattery, bribes, and the poor distribution I have made of the goods I possessed; by rejecting and mistreating the poor while distributing the goods entrusted to my care; by distressing those over whom I held authority, by not visiting prisoners, by denying the dead a burial, by not receiving the poor, and by neither feeding the hungry nor giving drink to the thirsty. I have sinned by never keeping the Sabbath or the other feast days, by not living purely and piously on those days, by readily giving in to those who encouraged me to do evil, by hurting instead of helping those who asked me for aid, by refusing to listen to the cries of the poor, by disrespecting the elderly, by breaking my word, by disobeying my parents, and by being ungrateful toward those from whom I received kindness. I have sinned by indulging in sensual pleasures, by disrespectful behavior in the Temple of God and making inappropriate gestures there, by entering it without reverence, and by engaging in vain and useless talk while inside; by despising the sacred vessels of the temple, by mocking the holy ceremonies, by touching and eating the sacred bread with impure lips and unholy <note>original: "profane"</note> hands, and by neglecting my prayers and worship.
> 
> I also detest the crimes I have committed through evil thoughts, vain and impure meditations, false suspicions, and hasty judgments; by the evil consent I have readily given to the advice of the wicked; by the lust for impure and sensual pleasures; by my idle words, my lies, and my deceit; by my various false vows; and by my constant slander and malicious gossip <note>original: "calumny"</note>.
> 
> I also detest the crimes I have committed within myself: the treachery and conflict <note>original: "discord"</note> I have stirred up; my curiosity, greed, lying, violence, cursing <note>original: "malediction"</note>, grumbling <note>original: "murmurs"</note>, blasphemies, empty words, insults, and deceit <note>original: "dissimulations"</note>. I detest my sins against God by breaking the Ten Commandments, by neglecting my duties and obligations, and by my lack of love toward God and toward my neighbor.
> 
> Furthermore, I hate the sins I have committed through all my senses: by sight, hearing, taste, smell, and touch, in every way that—

<summary>This page contains a detailed ritual confession required of the practitioner (Exorcist) before beginning magical operations. It lists a comprehensive array of moral and religious failings, emphasizing the need for total spiritual purification.</summary>
<keywords>Key of Solomon, Exorcist, Confession, Seven Deadly Sins, purification ritual, Ten Commandments, repentance, ritual magic</keywords>

[...and all the ways in which] human weakness can offend the Creator; by my carnal thoughts, deeds, and meditations.

In this, I humbly confess that I have sinned, and I recognize myself in the sight of God as the most guilty of all men.

I accuse myself before You, O God, and I adore You with all humility. O you, Holy Angels, and you, Children of God, in your presence I declare <note>original: "publish"</note> my sins, so that my Enemy <note>The Devil</note> may have no advantage over me and may not be able to reproach me on the last day. I speak so that he may not be able to say that I have concealed my sins, and that I may not be accused in the presence of the Lord; but, on the contrary, that on my account there may be joy in Heaven, as there is over the righteous who have confessed their sins in Your presence.

O Most Mighty and All-Powerful Father, grant through Your unbounded mercy that I may both see and know all the spirits which I summon <note>original: "invoke"</note>, so that through them I may see my will and desire accomplished. I ask this by Your sovereign grandeur, and by Your indescribable <note>original: "Ineffable"</note> and eternal glory, You who are and who will be forever the pure and indescribable Father of All.

Once the confession has been finished with great humility and with sincere feeling from the heart, the Master <note>The lead practitioner or exorcist</note> will recite the following prayer:

->PRAYER.<-

> O Lord All-Powerful, Eternal God and Father of all creatures, pour out the divine influence of Your mercy upon me, for I am Your creature. I beg You to defend me from my enemies and to strengthen within me a true and steadfast faith.
>
> O Lord, I commit my body and my soul to You, seeing as I put my trust in no one besides You; it is on You alone that I rely. O Lord my God, aid me; O Lord, hear me in the day and hour when I shall call upon You. I pray through Your mercy that You do not leave me in forgetfulness <note>original: "oblivion"</note>, nor remove me from Your presence. O Lord, be my help <note>original: "succor"</note>, You who are the God of my salvation. O Lord, create a new heart in me according to Your loving-kindness. These, O Lord, are the gifts which I wait for from You, O my God and my Master, You who live and reign for ever and ever <note>original: "unto the Ages of the Ages," a translation of the Latin *in saecula saeculorum*</note>. Amen.
>
> O Lord God, the All-Powerful One, who has formed for Yourself great and indescribable wisdom, which is co-eternal with You before the countless ages; You who at the birth of time created the heavens, the earth, the sea, and all things they contain; You who have given life <note>original: "vivified"</note> to all things by the breath of Your mouth, I praise You, I bless You, I adore You, and I glorify You. Be merciful <note>original: "propitious"</note> to me, for I am only a miserable sinner; do not despise me, but save and help me, for I am the work of Your hands. I appeal to <note>original: "conjure"</note> and entreat You by Your Holy Name to banish the darkness of ignorance from my spirit and to enlighten me with the fire of Your wisdom. Take away all evil desires from me, and do not let my speech be like that of the foolish. O You, the Living God, whose glory, honor, and kingdom shall extend for ever and ever. Amen.

## BOOK ONE.

### CHAPTER V.

#### PRAYERS AND CONJURATIONS.

#### PRAYER.

O LORD God, Holy Father, Almighty and Merciful One, Who have created all things, Who know all things and can do all things, from Whom nothing is hidden, to Whom nothing is impossible; You who know that we perform not these ceremonies to tempt Your power, but so that we may penetrate into the knowledge of hidden things; we pray You by Your Sacred Mercy to cause and to permit, that we may arrive at this understanding of secret things, of whatever nature they may be, by Your aid, O Most Holy ADONAI <note>original: "ADONAI"; a Hebrew name for God meaning "My Lord"</note>, Whose Kingdom and Power shall have no end throughout the Ages of Ages. Amen.

The Prayer being finished, let the Exorcist <term>Exorcist: In this context, the lead practitioner who commands or "exorcises" spirits into appearance, rather than one who drives them away</term> lay his hand upon the Pentacles <note>original: "Pentacles"; these are sacred symbols or talismans, often drawn on parchment or engraved on metal, representing spiritual forces</note>, while one of the Disciples shall hold open before him the Book wherein are written the prayers and conjurations proper for conquering, subduing, and rebuking the Spirits. Then the Master, turning towards each Quarter <note>The four cardinal directions: North, South, East, and West</note> of the Earth, and raising his eyes to Heaven, shall say:

> O Lord, be a strong tower of refuge for me, from the sight and assaults of the Evil Spirits.

After which let him turn again towards the Four Quarters of the Earth, and towards each let him utter the following words:

> Behold the Symbols and Names of the Creator, which give you terror and fear forever. Obey then, by the virtue of these Holy Names, and by these Mysteries of Mysteries.

After this he will see the Spirits come from every side. But in case they are occupied in some other place, or that they cannot come, or that they are unwilling to come: then let him begin again to invoke them in the following manner, and let the Exorcist be assured that even if they were bound with chains of iron and with fire, they could not refrain from coming to accomplish his will.

#### THE CONJURATION.*

O you Spirits, I conjure you by the Power, Wisdom, and Virtue of the Spirit of God, by the uncreated Divine Knowledge, by the vast Mercy of

<note>* There is an Invocation bearing the title of “The Qabalistical Invocation of Solomon,” given by Eliphas Lévi <note>A famous 19th-century French occultist and author</note>, which differs in many points from the one given above, though resembling it in some particulars. Lévi’s is more evidently constructed on the plan indicated in the “Siphra Dtzenioutha” <note>original: "Siphra Dtzenioutha"; Latinized Hebrew for "The Book of Concealment," a section of the Zohar, a fundamental work of Jewish mysticism</note>, Chapter III; Annotation § 5, sub-section 8, 9; while the one above more closely follows that laid down in section 5, sub-section 3 of the same work. I see no reason to suppose that Lévi’s is inauthentic. It will be noted by the Qabalistical reader, that the above Conjuration repeats the Divine Names associated with the Ten Sephiroth <note>The ten attributes or emanations in the Qabalah through which the Infinite reveals itself</note>.</note>

<page-num>23</page-num>

<header>THE KEY OF SOLOMON.</header>

God, by the strength of God, by the greatness of God, by the unity of God; and by the holy name of God **I AM** <note>original: "EHEIEH"; the Hebrew name of God meaning 'I am that I am,' found in Exodus 3:14</note>, which is the root, trunk, source, and origin of all the other Divine Names, and from which they all draw their life and their power. Adam invoked this name and acquired the knowledge of all created things.

I conjure <term>conjure: in this context, to command or summon a spirit using the authority of a sacred name</term> you by the indivisible name **YOD** <note>original: "IOD"; the first letter of the Divine Name in Hebrew, representing the unity and simplicity of God's nature</note>, which marks and expresses the simplicity and the unity of the Divine Nature. Abel invoked this name and deserved <note>In the original French manuscript, the phrase is "merita d'échapper."</note> to escape from the hands of his brother Cain.

I conjure you by the name **The Four-Letter Name of God** <note>original: "TETRAGRAMMATON ELOHIM"; 'Tetragrammaton' refers to the four-letter name of God, YHVH, often combined here with 'Elohim' (God)</note>, which expresses and signifies the grandeur of such a lofty Majesty. Noah pronounced this name and saved himself, protecting his entire household from the waters of the Great Flood.

I conjure you by the name of God **The Strong** <note>original: "EL"; a Hebrew name meaning 'God,' often implying strength or might</note>, the Strong and Wonderful, which denotes the mercy and goodness of His Divine Majesty. Abraham invoked this name and was found worthy to come out from Ur of the Chaldeans.

I conjure you by the most powerful name of **God the Mighty** <note>original: "ELOHIM GIBOR"; literally 'God of Strength'</note>, which shows the strength of an All-Powerful God who punishes the crimes of the wicked. He is a God who seeks out and punishes the sins of the fathers upon the children even to the third and fourth generation. Isaac invoked this name and was found worthy to escape from the sword of his father Abraham.

I conjure you and I exorcise <term>exorcise: to formally command or bind a spirit through a sacred ritual or name</term> you by the most holy name of **God the Knower** <note>original: "ELOAH VA-DAATH"; meaning 'God and Knowledge'</note>, which Jacob invoked when he was in great trouble. He was then found worthy to bear the name Israel—which means 'Vanquisher of God'—and he was delivered from the fury of his brother Esau.

I conjure you by the most potent name of **God the Lord of Armies** <note>original: "EL ADONAI TZABAOTH"; 'Tzabaoth' is Hebrew for 'Hosts' or 'Armies'</note>, <note>More usually the name TETRAGRAMMATON TZABAOTH is attributed to the seventh Sephira <term>Sephira: one of the ten emanations or attributes of God in the Kabbalah</term>.</note> who is the God of Armies ruling in the Heavens. Joseph invoked this name and was found worthy to escape from the hands of his brothers.

I conjure you by the most potent name of **God of Hosts** <note>original: "ELOHIM TZABAOTH"</note>, which expresses the piety, mercy, splendor, and knowledge of God. Moses invoked this name, and he was found worthy to deliver the people of Israel from Egypt and from the slavery of Pharaoh.

I conjure you by the most potent name of **The Almighty** <note>original: "SHADDAI"; a Hebrew name for God often translated as 'The Almighty' or 'Self-Sufficient'</note>, which signifies doing good to everyone. Moses invoked this name, and after he struck the sea, it divided into two parts in the middle, to the right hand and to the left. I conjure you by the most holy name of **The Living God** <note>original: "EL CHAI"</note>, <note>Both this name and 'The Almighty' (Shaddai) are attributed to the ninth Sephira; I have therefore placed the two invocations in the same paragraph.</note> which is the name of the Living God, through whose power our alliance and redemption were made. Moses invoked this name, and all the waters returned to their previous state and swallowed the Egyptians, so that not one of them escaped to carry the news back to the land of Egypt <note>original: "Mizraim"</note>.

Lastly, I conjure you all, you rebellious spirits, by the most holy name...

...of God **Lord King** <note>original: "ADONAI MELEKH"; a Hebrew title for God emphasizing His sovereignty</note>, whom Joshua invoked when he stayed the course of the Sun in his presence, through the power of **Metatron** <note>original: "Methratton"; in mystical tradition, the highest of archangels and the celestial scribe, often called the "Prince of Countenances"</note>, His primary image; and by the troops of Angels who never cease to cry out day and night, "**Holy, Holy, Holy, Lord God of Hosts, Heaven and Earth are full of Your Glory**" <note>original: "QADOSCH, SADOSCH, QADOSCH, ADONAI ELOHIM TZABAOTH"</note>; and by the Ten Angels who preside over the Ten **Emanations** <note>original: "Sephiroth"; the ten attributes in Kabbalah through which the Infinite reveals itself</note>, through whom God communicates and extends His influence over lower things, which are: **The Crown, Wisdom, Understanding, Mercy, Severity, Beauty, Victory, Splendor, Foundation, and Kingdom** <note>original: "KETHER, CHOKMAH, BINAH, GEDULAH, GEBURAH, TIPHER-ETH, NETZACH, HOD, YESOD, AND MALKUTH"</note>.

I command you once again, O Spirits, by all the Names of God, and by all His marvelous works; by the heavens; by the earth; by the sea; by the depth of the Abyss, and by that firmament which the very Spirit of God has moved; by the sun and by the stars; by the waters and by the seas, and all which they contain; by the winds, the whirlwinds, and the tempests; by the power of all herbs, plants, and stones; by all which is in the heavens, upon the earth, and in all the Abysses of the Shadows.

I command you once again, and I powerfully urge you, O Demons, in whatever part of the world you may be, so that you shall be unable to remain in the air, fire, water, earth, or in any part of the universe, or in any pleasant place which may attract you; but that you come promptly to accomplish our desire, and all things that we demand from your obedience.

I command you once again by the two Tables of the Law <note>The Ten Commandments given to Moses</note>, by the five books of Moses, by the Seven Burning Lamps on the Golden Candlestick before the face of the Throne of the Majesty of God, and by the Holy of Holies where the **High Priest** <note>original: "KOHEN HA-GADUL"</note> alone was permitted to enter.

I command you by Him Who has made the heavens and the earth, and who has measured those heavens in the hollow of His hand, and enclosed the earth with three of His fingers; Who is seated upon the **Cherubim** <note>original: "Kerubim"; a high order of angels</note> and upon the **Seraphim** <note>another high order of angels associated with light and fire</note>; and by the **Cherub** <note>original: "Kerub"</note> which God appointed and placed to guard the Tree of Life, armed with a flaming sword, after Man had been driven out of Paradise.

I command you once again, you who have rebelled against God, by Him Who alone has performed great wonders; by the Heavenly Jerusalem; and by the Most Holy Name of God in Four Letters <note>The Tetragrammaton, YHVH</note>, and by Him Who enlightens all things and shines upon all things by His Venerable and Ineffable Name, **I Am That I Am** <note>original: "EHEIEH ASHER EHEIEH"</note>; that you come immediately to execute our desire, whatever it may be.

I command you, and I order you absolutely, O Demons, in whatever part of the Universe you may be, by the power of all these Holy Names: **Lord, The Eternal, He, The Strong, God, Our God, The Triune God, I Am, Master** <note>original: "ADONAI, YAH, HOA, EL, ELOHA, ELOHINU, ELOHIM, EHEIEH, MARON"</note>...

\* The Archangel, who is also called the Prince of Countenances.
† I have made these Names as correct as possible; as in all the original manuscripts, the Hebrew is much mutilated. These names are some of them ordinary titles of God; others are Magical and Kabbalistic names compounded from the initials of sentences, etc.; and others are variations of other names.

# THE KEY OF SOLOMON

**The Palm of the Hand** <note>original: "Kaphu"</note>, **Fire** <note>original: "Esch"</note>, **He Shall Endure** <note>original: "Innon"</note>, **Power** <note>original: "Aven"</note>, **You are Mighty Forever, O Lord** <note>original: "Agla"; a Hebrew acronym often used in protective magic</note>, **The Enclosure** <note>original: "Hazor"</note>, **Truth** <note>original: "Emeth"</note>, **God** <note>original: "Yaii"</note>, **One is His Beginning, One is His Individuality, His Permutation is One** <note>original: "Araritha"; a complex mystical acronym</note>, **The Lord** <note>original: "Yova"</note>, **The Mighty One** <note>original: "Ha-Kabir"</note>, **The Messiah** <note>original: "Messiach"</note>, **The Dove** <note>original: "Ionah"</note>, **The King** <note>original: "Mal-Ka"</note>, **The Holy Angel** <note>original: "Erel"</note>, **God is My Strength** <note>original: "Kuzu, Matzpatz"; these are specialized cipher-names for the Name of God used in Kabbalah</note>, **God Almighty** <note>original: "El Shaddai"</note>; and by all the holy names of God which have been written with blood in the sign of an eternal covenant <note>original: "alliance"</note>.

I conjure you again by these other names of God, most holy and unknown, by the power of which names you tremble every day:—**Blessed** <note>original: "Baruc"</note>, **Bacurabon, Patacel, Alcheeghel, Aquachai, Homorion, I Am Who I Am** <note>original: "Eheieh"</note>, **The Destroyer** <note>original: "Abbaton"</note>, **Chevon, Cebon, Oyzroymas, Living** <note>original: "Chai"</note>, **I Am Who I Am** <note>original: "Eheieh"</note>, **Albamachi, Ortagu, Nale, Powerful King** <note>original: "Abelech" or "Helech"</note>, **Yeze** (or **Sechezze**); I command that you come quickly and without any delay into our presence from every quarter and every climate of the world where you may be, to execute all that we shall command you in the Great Name of God.

<note>original: "I give these Names as they stand, they do not all appear to be Hebrew; some of them suggest the style of the barbarous names in the Græco-Egyptian Magical Papyri." — These "barbarous names" are strings of sounds or ancient words used in rituals for their phonetic power rather than their literal meaning.</note>

# BOOK ONE

## CHAPTER VI

### A STRONGER AND MORE POWERFUL CONJURATION

If the spirits then appear immediately, it is well. If not, let the Master uncover the consecrated *Pentacles* <term>Pentacles: Talismans or medals inscribed with magical symbols and names of God used to protect the magician and command spirits.</term> which he should have made to restrict and command the spirits, and which he should wear fastened around his neck. Holding the medals (or pentacles) in his left hand and the consecrated knife in his right, and encouraging his companions, he shall say with a loud voice:

->ADDRESS<-

> Here are the symbols of secret things: the standards, the emblems, and the banners of God the Conqueror; and the weapons of the Almighty One to compel the spirits of the air <note>original: "Aerial Potencies"</note>. I command you absolutely by their power and strength <note>original: "virtue"</note> that you come near us, into our presence, from whatever part of the world you may be in, and that you do not delay in obeying us in all things that we shall command you by the power of God the Mighty One. Come promptly, do not delay to appear, and answer us with humility.

If they appear at this time, show them the pentacles and receive them with kindness, gentleness, and courtesy. Reason and speak with them, question them, and ask them for everything you intended to demand.

But if, on the contrary, they still do not make their appearance, hold the consecrated knife in your right hand, and—the pentacles being uncovered by the removal of their consecrated silk covering—strike and beat the air with the knife as if starting a fight. Comfort and encourage your companions, and then in a loud and stern voice repeat the following conjuration:

->CONJURATION<- <margin>This conjuration is almost identical to one found in the *Lemegeton*, or *Lesser Key*, a different work also attributed to Solomon.</margin>

> Here again I conjure you and most urgently command you. I force, restrict, and urge you to the utmost by the most mighty and powerful name of God, **The Mighty God** <note>original: "EL"</note>, strong and wonderful, and by God the Just and Upright. I exorcise <note>original: "exorcise" here means to solemnly charge or command a spirit</note> and command you that you in no way delay, but that you come immediately and in this instant here before us, without noise, deformity, or hideousness, but with all manner of gentleness and mildness.

> I exorcise you again and powerfully conjure you, commanding you with strength and violence by Him who spoke and it was done, and by all these names: **God** <note>original: "EL"</note>, **The Almighty** <note>original: "SHADDAI"</note>, **The Divine One** <note>original: "ELOHIM"</note>, **My God** <note>original: "ELOHI"</note>, **Of Hosts** <note>original: "TZABAOTH"</note>, **The Mighty Ones** <note>original: "ELIM"</note>, **I Am That I Am** <note>original: "ASHER EHEIEH"</note>, **The Eternal** <note>original: "YAH"</note>, **The Four-Letter Name** <note>original: "TETRAGRAMMATON"; the sacred name YHWH</note>, **The Almighty** <note>original: "SHADDAI"</note>, which signify God the High and...

...the Almighty, the God of Israel. Through Him, as we undertake all our operations, we shall prosper in all the works of our hands, seeing that the Lord is now, always, and forever with us, in our hearts and on our lips; and by His Holy Names, and by the power <note>original: "virtue"</note> of the Supreme God, we shall accomplish all our work.

Come at once, without any hideousness or deformity, into our presence. Come without any monstrous appearance, in a graceful form or figure. Come, for we conjure you with the utmost intensity by the Name **IAH** and **ON**, which Adam spoke and heard; by the Name **EL**, which Noah heard and so saved himself and his whole family from the Great Flood <note>original: "the Deluge"</note>; by the Name **IOD**, which Noah heard and through it knew God the Almighty One; by the Name **AGLA**, which Jacob heard when he saw the Ladder reaching to Heaven and the Angels who ascended and descended upon it, leading him to call that place the House of God and the Gate of Heaven; and by the Name **ELOHIM**, and in the Name **ELOHIM**, which Moses spoke, called upon, and heard at Horeb, the Mount of God, where he was found worthy to hear Him speak from the Burning Bush; and by the Name **AIN SOPH** <term>Ain Soph: A Hebrew term meaning "the Infinite" or "without end," referring to the divine essence before it manifested in creation.</term>, which Aaron heard and was immediately made eloquent, wise, and learned; and by the Name **TZABAOTH** <note>original: "Tzabaoth," a Hebrew title often translated as "Lord of Hosts" or "Lord of Armies."</note>, which Moses named and called upon, causing all the ponds and rivers throughout the land of Egypt to be covered with blood;* and by the name **IOD**, which Moses named and called upon, so that by striking the dust of the earth, both men and beasts were struck with disease;† and by the Name, and in the Name **PRIMEUMATON**, which Moses named and called upon, causing a great and severe hail to fall throughout all the land of Egypt, destroying the vines, the trees, and the woods of that country; and by the Name **IAPHAR**, which Moses heard and called upon, and immediately a great pestilence began to appear through all the land of Egypt, striking and killing the donkeys, the oxen, and the sheep of the Egyptians so that they all died; and by the Name **ABADDON**, which Moses called upon as he sprinkled dust toward heaven, causing such heavy rain to fall upon the men, cattle, and flocks that they all died throughout the land of Egypt; and by the Name **ELION** <term>Elion: Derived from the Hebrew "Elyon," meaning "Most High."</term>, which Moses called upon, bringing such a great hailstorm—the likes of which had never been seen since the beginning of the world—that all people, herds, and everything in the fields perished and died throughout all the land of Egypt. And by the Name **EDONAL**, which Moses called upon, bringing such a vast swarm of locusts to the land of Egypt that they devoured and swallowed up everything the hail had spared; and by the Name of **PATHEON**,‡ which he called upon to bring a darkness so thick, so awful, and so terrible throughout the land of Egypt for a period of three days and three nights that nearly everyone who remained alive died; and

‡ This is often written as **PATHTUMON** in similar conjurations, but the manuscripts used here all agree on this form.

By the Name **YESOD** and in the Name **YESOD**, which Moses called upon <note>original: "invoked"</note>, at midnight when all the first-born, of both humans and animals, died; and by the Name **YESHIMON**, which Moses named and called upon, so that the Red Sea divided and separated into two; and by the name **HESION**, which Moses called upon, so that all the army of Pharaoh was drowned in the waters; and by the Name **ANABONA**, which Moses heard upon Mount Sinai and was found worthy to receive the stone tablets written with the finger of God the Creator; and by the Name **ERYGION**, which Joshua called upon when he fought against the Moabites, defeating them and gaining the victory; and by the Name **HOA**, and in the Name **HOA**, which David called upon and was delivered from the hand of Goliath; and by the name **YOD**, which Solomon named and called upon, being found worthy to ask for and receive in sleep the unutterable <note>original: "Ineffable"</note> Wisdom of God; and by the Name **YIAI**, which Solomon named and called upon, being found worthy to have power over all the Demons, Potencies, Powers, and Virtues <term>Virtues: In this context, a specific rank or category of spirits or angels.</term> of the Air.

By these, then, and by all the other Names of God Almighty, Holy, Living, and True, we powerfully command you—you who by your own sin have been cast down from the highest heaven <note>original: "Empyreal Heaven"</note> and from before His Throne. By Him who has cast you down into the deepest parts of the Abysses of Hell, we command you boldly and resolutely; and by that terrible Day of the Sovereign Judgment of God, on which all the dry bones in the earth will arise to hear and listen to the Word of God with their bodies, and will present themselves before the face of God Almighty; and by that Last Fire which shall consume all things; by the Crystal Sea known to us, which is before the Face of God; by the indescribable <note>original: "indicible"</note> and unutterable power <note>original: "virtue"</note>, force, and strength of the Creator Himself, by His Almighty power, and by the Light and Flame which come from His Countenance and which are before His Face; by the Angelic Powers which are in the Heavens, and by the greatest Wisdom of Almighty God; by the Seal of David, by the Ring and Seal of Solomon, which was revealed to him by the Most High and Sovereign Creator; and by the Nine Medals or Pentacles <term>Pentacles: Magical diagrams or talismans used in rituals to constrain spirits or protect the practitioner.</term> which we have among our Symbols, which come from Heaven and are among the "Mysteries of Mysteries" or "Secrets of Secrets," which you can also see in my hand, consecrated and exorcised with the proper and required Ceremonies. By these, then, and by all the Secrets which the Almighty encloses in the treasures of the Sovereign and Highest Wisdom, by His Hand, and by His marvelous power, I conjure, force, and exorcise you to come without delay to perform in our presence whatever we shall command you.

I conjure you again by that most Holy Name which the whole Universe fears, respects, and reveres, which is written with these letters and characters: **IOD, HE, VAU, HE** <note>The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter name of God in Hebrew.</note>; and by the last and terrible judgment; by the Seat of **BALDACHIA**;* and by this Holy Name, **YIAI**, which Moses called upon, and—

<note>* Sometimes, though I think mistakenly, written as Bas-dathea. I believe the word means "Lord of Life."</note>

...there followed that great Judgment of God, when Dathan and Abiram were swallowed up in the center of the earth. <note>Dathan and Abiram were biblical figures who rebelled against Moses and were punished by being swallowed by the earth (Numbers 16).</note> Otherwise, if you oppose and resist us by your disobedience to the virtue and power of this Name **YIAI**, we curse you even to the depth of the Great Abyss, into which we shall cast, hurl, and bind you, if you show yourselves rebellious against the Secret of Secrets, and against the Mystery of Mysteries. **AMEN, AMEN. LET IT BE DONE, LET IT BE DONE.** <note>original: "FIAT, FIAT"</note>

This Conjuration you shall say and perform, turning yourself toward the East; and if they do not appear, you shall repeat it to the Spirits, turning toward the South, the West, and the North in succession, until you have repeated it four times. And if they still do not appear even then, you shall make the Sign of **TAU** <note>The letter Tau (T) was often used as a symbol of the cross or protection.</note> upon the foreheads of your companions, and you shall say:—

### CONJURATION.

Behold again the Symbol and the Name of a Sovereign and Conquering God, at which the entire Universe fears, trembles, and shudders, and through the most mysterious words of the Secret Mysteries and by their Virtue, Strength, and Power.

I conjure you again, I constrain and command you with the utmost intensity and power, by that most potent and powerful Name of God, **EL**, strong and wonderful; by Him who spoke and it was done; and by the Name **IAH**, which Moses heard and spoke with God; and by the Name **AGLA**, which Joseph called upon <note>original: "invoked"</note> and was delivered out of the hands of his brothers; and by the Name **VAU**, which Abraham heard and knew God the Almighty One; and by the Name of Four Letters, **TETRAGRAMMATON** <note>The sacred four-letter Hebrew name of God, often transliterated as YHWH.</note>, which Joshua named and called upon, and he was rendered worthy and found deserving to lead the Army of Israel into the Promised Land; and by the Name **ANABONA**, by which God formed Man and the whole Universe; and by the Name **ARPHETON**, and in the Name **ARPHETON**, by which the Angels who are destined for that purpose will summon the Universe in visible body and form, and will assemble all people together by the sound of the Trumpet at that terrible and awful Day of Judgment, when the memory of the wicked and ungodly shall perish; and by the Name **ADONAI**, by which God will judge all human flesh, at whose voice all men—both good and evil—will rise again, and all men and Angels will assemble in the air before the Lord, who will judge and condemn the wicked; and by the Name **ONEIPHETON**, by which God will summon the dead and raise them up again to life; and by the Name **ELOHIM**, and in the Name **ELOHIM**, by which God will disturb and stir up tempests throughout all the seas, so that they will cast out the fish from them, and in one day a third of the people near the sea and the rivers shall die; and by the Name—

**ELOHI**, * and in the Name **ELOHI**, by which God will dry up the sea and the rivers so that men can walk on foot through their dry beds; and by the Name **ON**, and in the Name **ON**, by which God shall restore and return the sea, the rivers, the streams, and the brooks to their previous state; and by the Name **MESSIACH**,† and in the Name **MESSIACH**, by which God will make all animals fight one another so that they shall die in a single day; and by the Name **ARIEL**, by which God shall destroy all buildings in a single day, so that not one stone shall be left upon another; and by the Name **IAHT**,‡ by which God will throw one stone upon another so that all people and nations will flee from the seashore and will say to them, "Cover us and hide us"; and by the Name **EMANUEL**, by which God will perform wonders, and the winged creatures and birds of the air shall contend with one another; and by the Name **ANAEL**,§ and in the Name **ANAEL**, by which God will cast down the mountains and fill up the valleys, so that the surface of the earth shall be level in all parts; and by the Name **ZEDEREZA**,|| and in the Name **ZEDEREZA**, by which God will cause the Sun and Moon to be darkened and the Stars of heaven to fall; and by the Name **SEPHERIEL**,¶ by which God will come to Universal Judgment, like a Prince newly crowned entering in triumph into his capital city, wearing a belt <note>original: "zone"</note> of gold and preceded by Angels, and at His appearance all regions and parts of the Universe shall be troubled and astonished, and a fire shall go out before Him, and flames and storms shall surround Him; and by the Name **TAU**,** by which God brought the Great Flood <note>original: "Deluge"</note>, and the waters rose above the mountains, even fifteen cubits above their summits; and by the Name **RUACHIAH**,†† by which God, having cleansed the Ages, will make His Holy Spirit descend upon the Universe and will cast you—you rebellious Spirits and unclean beings—into the depths of the Lake of the Abyss, in misery, filth, and mire, and will place you in impure and foul dungeons bound with eternal chains of fire.

By these Names then, and by all the other Holy Names of God before whom no man can stand and live—Names which the armies of the Demons fear, tremble at, and shudder—we conjure you, we powerfully exorcise and command you, conjuring you in addition by the terrible and tremendous PATHS‡‡ of GOD and by His Holy dwelling place where He reigns and commands throughout the eternal Ages. Amen.

# THE KEY OF SOLOMON

By the power of all these things mentioned before, we command you that you do not remain in any place where you currently are, but to come here promptly and without delay to do whatever we shall command you. But if you are still stubborn <note>original: "contumacious"</note>, we, by the authority of a sovereign and powerful God, strip you of every quality, condition, rank <note>original: "degree"</note>, and position which you now enjoy; we cast you down <note>original: "precipitate"</note> and banish you to the Kingdom of Fire and Sulphur, to be eternally tormented there. Come then from all parts of the earth, wherever you may be, and look upon the symbols and names of that triumphant Sovereign whom all creatures obey. Otherwise, we shall bind you and lead you into our presence against your will, bound with chains of fire, because the effects that come from our knowledge <note>original: "Science"</note> and ritual <note>original: "operation"</note> burn with a fire that shall consume and burn you eternally. For by these things the whole universe trembles, the earth is moved, its stones crash together, all creatures obey, and the rebellious spirits are tormented by the power of the *Sovereign Creator*.

It is then certain that they will come, even if they are bound with chains of fire, unless they are prevented by matters of the highest importance. In that case, however, they will send ambassadors and messengers through whom you shall easily and surely learn what is occupying the spirits and what they are doing. But if they still do not appear in answer to the conjuration above and remain disobedient, then let the Master of the Art (the Exorciser) stand up and encourage his companions to be of good heart and not to despair of the ritual's final success. Let him strike the air with the *Consecrated Knife* toward the four cardinal directions <note>original: "Four Quarters of the Universe"</note>, and then let him kneel in the middle of the circle, along with his companions in their assigned places. Let them say the following in a low voice along with him, turning toward the East:

->ADDRESS TO THE ANGELS.<-

> I call upon and pray to you, O Angels of God and Celestial Spirits, to come to my aid; come and look upon the signs of heaven, and be my witness before the Sovereign Lord regarding the disobedience of these evil and fallen spirits, who were once your companions.

Once this is done, let the Master stand up and compel them with a stronger conjuration in the following way.

->THE KEY OF SOLOMON.<-

# BOOK ONE.
## CHAPTER VII.
### AN EXTREMELY POWERFUL CONJURATION.

Behold us again prepared to conjure you by the Names and Symbols of God, with which we are fortified, and by the power of the Highest One. We command you and strongly ordain you by the most strong and powerful Names of God—who is worthy of all praise, admiration, honor, glory, worship <note>original: "veneration"</note>, and fear—that you delay no longer, but that you appear before us without any noise or disturbance. On the contrary, appear with great respect and courtesy, in a beautiful and human form.

If they then appear, let them see the Pentacles <note>A pentacle is a solar or planetary talisman used to command spirits and protect the magician.</note>, and say:

> Obey! Obey! Behold the Symbols and Names of the Creator; be gentle and peaceable, and obey in all things that we shall command you.

They will then immediately talk with you, as a friend speaks to a friend. Ask of them all that you desire, with persistence, firmness, and assurance, and they will obey you.

But if they do not appear yet, the Master should not lose his courage on 그 account, for there is nothing in the world stronger or of greater force to overawe the spirits than persistence. Let him, however, re-examine and correct <note>original: "reform"</note> the Circle, and let him take up a little dust from the earth, which he shall throw towards the four cardinal directions <note>original: "Four Quarters of the Universe"</note>; and having placed his knife upon the ground, let him say on his knees, turning toward the North:

> In the Name of the Lord God of Hosts, the Almighty <note>original: "ADONAI ELOHIM TZABAOTH SHADDAI"</note>, Lord God of Armies Almighty, may we successfully perform the works of our hands, and may the Lord be present with us in our hearts and on our lips.

After these words have been said while kneeling on the ground, the Master should shortly after arise and open his arms wide as if wishing to embrace the air, and say:

#### CONJURATION.

> By the Holy Names of God written in this Book, and by the other Holy and Unutterable <note>original: "Ineffable"</note> Names which are written in the Book of Life, we conjure you to come to us promptly and without any delay. Therefore do not linger, but appear in a beautiful and pleasing form and figure, by these Holy Names: Lord, Hosts, God, My God, God (the plural of majesty), the Almighty <note>original: "ADONAI, TZABAOTH, EL, ELOHI, ELOHIM, SHADDAI"</note>; and by "I Am That I Am" <note>original: "EHEIEH"</note>, and by the four letters Y-H-V-H <note>original: "YOD HE VAU HE"</note> which is the Great Name of God, the Tetragrammaton written with four letters, Unutterable <note>original: "ANAPHODITION"</note> and Inexpressible; by the God of those virtues and powers who lives in the Heavens, who rides upon the Cherubim <note>original: "Kerubim"</note>, who moves upon the wings of the wind—He whose power is in Heaven and on Earth, who spoke and it was done, who commanded and the whole Universe was created; and by the Holy Names and in the Holy Names, Yah, Yah, Yah <note>original: "IAH"</note>, Lord of Hosts; and by all the Names of God, the Living and the True, I repeat the conjuration, and I conjure you anew, you evil and rebellious spirits living in the depths of darkness.

<summary>This page details the ritual steps to take if spirits do not immediately appear, including the use of pentacles and a secondary, high-intensity conjuration utilizing various Hebrew names of God.</summary>

By that Ineffable Name <term>Ineffable Name: A name considered too sacred or powerful to be spoken aloud, often referring to the Tetragrammaton.</term> which Moses heard and invoked, which he received from God from the midst of the Burning Bush, we conjure you to obey our commands and to come to us promptly with all gentleness of manner.

Again we command you with intensity <note>original: vehemence</note>, and we exorcise you with persistence <note>original: constancy</note>, that you and all your comrades come to us in an agreeable and gracious manner like the breeze, to accomplish one after another <note>original: successively</note> our various commands and desires. We force and exorcise you by and in the admirable Name which was written on the Tables of Stone which God gave upon Mount Sinai; and by and in the wonderful Name which Aaron the High Priest wore <note>original: bare</note> written upon his breast, by which also God created the World, which name is AXINETON; and by the Living God Who is One throughout the Ages, whose dwelling is in the Ineffable Light, Whose Name is Wisdom, and Whose Spirit is Life, before Whom goes forth Fire and Flame, Who has from that Fire formed the firmament, the Stars, and the Sun; and Who with that Fire will burn you all forever, as well as all who shall go against <note>original: contravene</note> the Words of His Will.

We exorcise you anew by the application of these letters, by whose power burning fire is quenched and the whole universe trembles.

We constrain you yet again by the Seal of the Sun, which is the Word of God; and by the Seal of the Moon and of the Stars, we bind you. By the other animals and creatures which are in Heaven, by whose wings Heaven cleanses itself, we force and imperiously attract you to carry out our will without fail. We conjure, command, and powerfully <note>original: terribly</note> exorcise you to draw near to us without delay and without fear, as far as is possible for you, here before this circle as humble petitioners, gently and respectfully <note>original: with discretion</note>, to fulfill our will in all things and through all things. If you come promptly and voluntarily, you shall inhale our perfumes and our incense <note>original: suffumigations</note> of pleasant fragrance, which will be both agreeable and delightful to you. Furthermore, you will see the symbol of your Creator and the names of His holy angels, and we shall afterwards dismiss you and send you away with thanks. 

But if, on the contrary, you do not come quickly and you show yourselves stubborn <note>original: self-opinionated</note>, rebellious, and defiant <note>original: contumacious</note>, we shall conjure you again and exorcise you ceaselessly. We will repeat all the previously mentioned words and the holy names of God and of the holy angels, by which names we shall harass you. If that is not sufficient, we will add even greater and more powerful names; we will also add other names which you have not yet heard from us, which are those of Almighty God and which will make both you and your princes tremble and quake with fear. By those names, we conjure both you and them also, and we shall not stop our work until the fulfillment of our will. 

But if by chance you still harden yourselves and show yourselves stubborn, disobedient, rebellious, resistant <note>original: refractory</note>, and defiant, and if you still resist our powerful conjurations, we shall pronounce against you this decree of judgment <note>original: warrant of arrest</note> in the name of God Almighty, and this final sentence: that you shall fall into dangerous disease and leprosy; that as a sign of divine vengeance, you shall all perish by a terrifying and horrible death; that a fire shall consume and devour you on every side and utterly crush you; and that by the power of God, a flame shall go forth from His mouth which shall burn you up and reduce you to nothing in Hell. Therefore, do not delay in coming, for we shall not cease from these powerful conjurations until you are forced to appear against your will.

Thus, then, we conjure and exorcise you anew by and in the holy name of **On** <note>original: ON; a name of God often associated in medieval magic with the Greek 'the One' or 'Being'</note>, which is interpreted and called God; by the name and in the name of **Eheieh** <note>original: EHEIEH; Hebrew for 'I Am'</note>, which is the true name of God, "I am He who is"; by and in the unutterable <note>original: Ineffable</note> Name of Four Letters **Yod He Vav He** <note>The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter name of God</note>, the knowledge and understanding of which is hidden even from the angels; by the name and in the name of **El** <note>Hebrew for 'God' or 'Mighty One'</note>, which signifies and represents the powerful and consuming fire that comes from His face <note>original: Countenance</note> and which shall be your ruin and destruction; and by the light of the angels which is kindled and taken unutterably from that flame of divine passion.

By these, then, and by other most holy names which we pronounce against you from the bottom of our hearts, do we force and compel you.

...if you are still rebellious and disobedient. We conjure you powerfully and strongly exorcise you, so that you come to us with joy and speed, without fraud or deceit, in truth and not in error.

Come then, come; behold the signs and the names of your Creator; behold the holy pentacles <note>sacred geometric symbols used in magic to constrain spirits</note> by whose power the Earth is moved, and its trees and the abysses tremble. Come; come; come.

Once these things have been performed, you will see the spirits come from all sides in great haste with their princes and superiors. The spirits of the first order will appear like soldiers, armed with spears, shields, and breastplates <note>original: corslets</note>. Those of the second order will appear like barons, princes, dukes, captains, and generals of armies. For the third and final order, their king will appear; preceding him are many musicians playing instruments, accompanied by beautiful and melodious voices singing in chorus.

Then the exorcist, or Master of the Art, upon the arrival of the king (whom he will see crowned with a diadem), should uncover the holy pentacles and medals which he wears upon his breast—which had been covered with a cloth of silk or fine twined linen—and show them to him, saying:

> Behold the signs and holy names by and before whose power every knee should bow—of all who are in heaven, on earth, or in hell. Humble yourselves, therefore, under the mighty hand of God.

Then the king will bow his knee before you and will say, “What do you wish, and why have you caused us to come here from the infernal <note>meaning: the underworld or hell</note> regions?”

Then the exorcist, or Master of the Magical Art, with a confident air and a grave and commanding voice, shall order and command him to be calm, to keep the rest of his attendants peaceful, and to impose silence upon them.

Let him also renew his fumigations <note>the ritual burning of incense</note> and offer large quantities of incense, which he should immediately place upon the fire in order to appease the spirits as he promised them. He should then cover the pentacles, and he will see wonderful things that are impossible to describe, concerning worldly matters and all sciences.

When this is finished, let the Master uncover the pentacles and demand everything he wishes from the king of the spirits; it is the same even if there are only one or two spirits present. Having obtained all his desire, he shall give them permission to leave <note>original: license them to depart</note> as follows:

### THE PERMISSION TO DEPART.

> In the name of Adonai <note>a Hebrew name for God meaning "My Lord"</note>, the Eternal and Everlasting One, let each of you return to your place; let there be peace between us and you, and be ready to come when you are called.

After this, he should recite the first chapter of Genesis: “In the beginning, God created...” <note>original Hebrew: Berashith Bara Elohim</note>.

Once this is done, let them all leave the circle in order, one after the other, with the Master going first. Furthermore, let them wash their faces with the...

Cleanse yourselves with exorcised water <note>spiritually purified water used in rituals to remove impurities</note>, as will be explained later, and then let them put on their regular clothes and go about their business.

Take notice and observe carefully that this last conjuration is of such great importance and effectiveness that even if the spirits were bound with chains of iron and fire, shut up in some strong fortress, or held by an oath, they still could not delay their arrival. However, if they were being summoned in some other part of the universe by another practitioner <note>original: Exorcist or Master of the Art</note> using the same conjuration, the Master should add to his command that they should at least send him some messengers, or a representative to tell him where they are, what they are doing, and the reason why they cannot come and obey him.

But if (which is almost impossible) they are still stubborn and disobedient, refusing to obey, their names should be written on virgin paper <note>parchment or paper that has never been used for any other purpose</note>, which the Master should defile with mud, dust, or clay. Then he shall start a fire with dry rue <note>a bitter herb used for protection and purification</note>, upon which he shall put powdered asafoetida <note>a pungent resin with a very strong, unpleasant smell, used here to drive away or punish spirits</note> and other foul-smelling things. After this, let him place the aforementioned names, written on parchment or *Virgin Parchment Paper*, into the fire, saying:

### THE CONJURATION OF THE FIRE.

I summon you, O Creature of Fire, by Him who moves the Earth and makes it tremble, that you burn and torment these spirits so that they feel it intensely, and that they may be burned by you eternally.

This being said, you shall throw the aforementioned paper into the fire, saying:

### THE CURSE.

Be accursed, damned, and eternally rejected. May you be tormented with perpetual pain, so that you find no rest by night or by day, nor for a single moment, if you do not immediately obey the command of Him who makes the universe tremble. We curse you by these names—names which, when spoken and invoked, cause all creatures to obey and tremble with fear and terror; names which can turn aside lightning and thunder, and which will utterly destroy and banish you. These names are:
Aleph, Beth, Gimel, Daleth, He, Vau, Zayin, Cheth, Teth, Yod, Kaph, Lamed, Mem, Nun, Samekh, Ayin, Pe, Tzaddi, Qoph, Resh, Shin, Tau. <note>These are the names of the letters of the Hebrew alphabet, which are believed to hold mystical power.</note>

Therefore, by these secret names and by these signs which are full of mysteries, we curse you. By the power of the Three Principles—Air, Water, and Fire <note>original: Aleph, Mem, Shin</note>—we strip you of all office and dignity you have enjoyed until now. By their virtue and power, we banish you into a lake of sulfur and flame, and into the deepest depths of the abyss, to burn there forever.

Then they will surely come without any delay and in great haste, crying out: "O our Lord and Prince, deliver us from this suffering."

During this entire time, you should have an exorcised pen, paper, and ink ready near you, as will be described later. Write their names again and light a new fire. Place benzoin <note>original: gum benjamin; a balsamic resin used in incense</note>, frankincense <note>original: olybdanum</note>, and storax <note>a fragrant plant resin</note> on the fire to create a ritual smoke <note>original: fumigation</note>. With these scents, you shall perfume the paper containing the names; however, you should have these names prepared in advance. Then show them the Holy Pentacles <note>sacred geometric symbols used to constrain spirits</note> and ask of them whatever you wish, and you shall obtain it. Having achieved your purpose, dismiss the spirits by saying:

### THE PERMISSION TO DEPART <note>original: THE LICENSE TO DEPART</note>

> By the power of these *Pentacles*, and because you have been obedient and followed the commandments of the Creator, feel and inhale this pleasing scent. Afterward, depart to your homes and retreats. Let there be peace between us; be ever ready to come whenever you are summoned and called. May the blessing of God, as far as you are capable of receiving it, be upon you, provided you are obedient and prompt to come to us without the need for solemn rites and ceremonies on our part.

You should also create a book made of Virgin Parchment Paper, and in it write the previously mentioned conjurations. Compel the demons to swear upon this book that they will come whenever they are called and present themselves before you whenever you wish to consult them. Afterward, you can cover this book with sacred symbols <note>original: Sigils</note> on a plate of silver, and write or engrave the *Holy Pentacles* within it. You may open this book on either Sundays or Thursdays, preferably at night rather than during the day, and the spirits will appear.

Regarding the term "night," understand it to mean the night following the day mentioned, and not the night before. And remember that during the day, the demons are ashamed, for they are *creatures of darkness* <note>original: Animals of Darkness</note>.

* This book can be made from Genuine Virgin Parchment Paper, which may be obtained from Messrs. de Laurence, Scott & Co. This book may consist of sixteen pages and can be made by stitching one sheet of Genuine Virgin Parchment Paper (which comes in eight pieces) with black silk thread. You may do the stitching yourself. See Order No. 292 in the Messrs. de Laurence, Scott & Co. catalogue.

The student who reads this should understand that there are many kinds of so-called "parchment paper" sold, but only one true kind of parchment paper exists, and that is *Genuine Virgin Parchment*. This is made from the skin of stillborn <note>original: dead born</note> lambs and is always used for creating *talismans*, *charms*, and *pentacles*. The ancient astrologers and the old masters of *talismanic magic* never used anything other than *Genuine Virgin Parchment*, as it is known to be the only material that meets the requirements of *occultism* and *talismanic magic*.

*Genuine Virgin Parchment*, made from the skin of stillborn lambs, is very expensive, rare, and difficult to obtain. It must first be polished with pumice stone with great effort and expense before its surface is fit to be engraved upon or before any drawings can be made on it. Many foolishly hesitate to pay the price asked for a sheet of *Genuine Virgin Parchment*, much preferring to pay less for worthless material; but gold is gold, diamonds are diamonds, and *Virgin Parchment* is Virgin Parchment. Just as gold is always worth its weight in money, and diamonds are always worth their price per carat, so *Genuine Virgin Parchment Paper* is always worth its price per sheet.

Messrs. de Laurence, Scott & Co. are the only firm, as far as the writer knows, that is able to provide the pure, unspotted, *Genuine Virgin Parchment Paper* made from the skin of stillborn lambs. The cost for a sheet is not high considering its great value and the secret purposes for which it can be used. Whether you choose to pay the price or not is entirely your own concern.

# BOOK ONE.

## CHAPTER VIII.

### CONCERNING PENTACLES, AND THE MANNER OF CONSTRUCTING THEM.

As we have already mentioned the *Pentacles* <note>sacred geometric symbols or seals used in ritual magic to command spirits or provide protection</note>, it is necessary for you to understand that the entire science and understanding of our *"Key"* depends on the operation, knowledge, and use of these *Pentacles*.

Therefore, anyone who wishes to perform any operation using these *Medals* or *Pentacles* and become an expert in them must follow what has been previously ordered. Let him then, *O my son Rehoboam* <note>original: Roboam; the son of King Solomon in the Bible</note>, know and understand that in the aforementioned *Pentacles* he shall find those Ineffable <note>too sacred to be spoken aloud</note> and Most Holy Names which were written by the finger of God on the *Tablets of Moses*. These I, SOLOMON, have received through the service of an Angel by Divine Revelation. I have collected, arranged, consecrated, and kept them for the benefit of the human race and the preservation of the *Body* and the *Soul*.

The Pentacles should be made on the days and hours of Mercury <note>traditionally Wednesdays</note>, when the Moon is in an *aerial* or terrestrial sign; she should also be waxing <note>original: in her increase</note>, and have been above the horizon for the same number of days as the Sun.

It is necessary to have a chamber or cabinet specifically set apart and newly cleaned where you can remain without interruption. Having entered this room with your companions, you shall incense and perfume it with the scents and perfumes of the Art. The sky should be clear and calm. It is necessary that you have one or more pieces of virgin paper <note>paper or parchment that has never been used for any other purpose</note> prepared and arranged, as we shall explain more fully later in the proper place.

You shall begin writing or constructing the *Pentacles* in the hour mentioned above. Among other things, you shall primarily use these colors: gold, cinnabar <note>a bright red mineral</note> or vermilion red, and sky-blue or brilliant azure. Furthermore, you shall make these *Medals* or *Pentacles* with an exorcised pen and consecrated colors, as we shall show you later. Whenever you construct them, it is better if you can complete them within the same hour you began. However, if it is absolutely necessary to interrupt the work, you should wait for the correct day and hour before starting again.

Once the *Pentacles* are finished and completed, take a cloth of very fine silk (as we shall instruct you later) and wrap the Pentacles in it. After this, take a large earthenware vessel filled with charcoal, upon which you must place frankincense, mastic <note>a resin from the mastic tree</note>, and aloes <note>a fragrant wood or resin used as incense</note>, all of which must have been previously conjured and exorcised as will be explained later. You must also be pure, clean, and washed, according to the instructions given in the proper place. Furthermore, you should have the sickle or knife of magical art, with which you shall draw a circle and trace an inner circle within it; in the space between the—

---

\* *meaning* in Gemini, Libra, Aquarius, Taurus, Virgo, or Capricorn. <note>These represent the Air and Earth signs of the zodiac.</note>
