<header>THE KEY OF SOLOMON.</header>

In the two <note>referring to the circles or borders of the Pentacle</note> you shall write the Names of God <note>original: preferably those having some reference to the work in hand</note> which you think fit and proper. After this, it is necessary that you should have within the Circle a vessel of earth with burning coals and fragrant perfumes placed upon them. With these, you shall cense <note>original: fumigate</note> the aforementioned *Pentacles*; and, having turned your face towards the East, you shall hold the said *Pentacles* over the smoke of the *Temple Incense*, and shall repeat devoutly the following *Psalms of David my Father*: Psalms 8, 21, 27, 29, 32, 51, 72, and 134. <note>The author notes these numbers follow the English translations of the Bible rather than the Hebrew numbering.</note>

(For a convenient form of the Circle which may be used for preparing instruments and other similar items, as well as for consecrating the *Pentacles*, see *Figure 3*.)

*After this, you shall repeat the following Oration:*

->THE ORATION.<-

O ADONAI <note>Hebrew: "My Lord"</note> most powerful, EL <note>Hebrew: "God"</note> most strong, AGLA <note>a Qabalistic acronym for 'Atah Gibor Le-olam Adonai'—'Thou art mighty forever, O Lord'</note> most holy, ON <note>a name for God often used in medieval magic, likely derived from a Greek word for 'Being'</note> most righteous, the ALEPH and the TAU <note>the first and last letters of the Hebrew alphabet, signifying 'the Beginning and the End'; the Qabalistic word AZOTH may also be used here</note>, the Beginning and the End; You Who have established all things in Your Wisdom; You Who have chosen Abraham Your faithful servant, and have promised that in his seed shall all nations of the earth be blessed, a seed which You have multiplied as the Stars of Heaven; You Who appeared to Your servant Moses in flame in the midst of the *Burning Bush*, and made him walk with dry feet through the Red Sea; You Who gave the Law to him upon Mount Sinai; You Who have granted to SOLOMON Your Servant these *Pentacles* by Your great Mercy, for the preservation of Soul and Body; we most humbly implore and supplicate Your Holy Majesty, that these *Pentacles* may be consecrated by Your power, and prepared in such a manner that they may obtain virtue <note>meaning efficacy or spiritual power</note> and strength against all Spirits, through You, O Most Holy ADONAI, Whose Kingdom, Empire, and sovereignty remains and endures without end.

When these words have been said, you shall perfume the *Pentacles* with the same sweet scents and perfumes. Afterward, having wrapped them in a piece of prepared silk cloth, you shall put them in a fit and clean place, which you may open whenever it pleases you and close again at your pleasure and according to your will. We will hereafter show you the method and manner of preparing this place, of perfuming it with scents and sweet odors, and of sprinkling it with the Water and *Water-Sprinkler* <note>original: Aspergillum</note> *of Magical Art*; for all these things contain many good properties and innumerable virtues, as experience will easily teach you.

We have already said enough regarding the *Solemn Conjuration of Spirits*.

We have also spoken enough in our present Key regarding the manner in which it is necessary to attract the Spirits so as to make them speak. Now, by Divine aid, I will teach you how to perform certain experiments with success.

<header>THE KEY OF SOLOMON.</header>

Know, <note>original: From here to the end of the chapter is only found in the Lansdowne Manuscripts 1203, a 17th-century French manuscript held in the British Library.</note> O my son Roboam, that all the Divine <term>Sigils</term> <note>symbolic icons or signatures representing spiritual entities or forces</note>, <term>Characters</term> <note>specific magical symbols or scripts</note>, and Names (which are the most precious and excellent things in Nature, whether earthly or heavenly), should be written by you each separately. This must be done when you are in a state of grace and purity, upon <term>Virgin parchment</term> <note>animal skin prepared for writing that has never been used before</note>, with ordinary ink, in the beginning of the month of August <note>original: That is, when the sun is in the astrological sign of Leo.</note> before sunrise, while raising your eyes toward heaven and turning toward the east. You shall keep them to hang from your neck—whichever one you wish—on the day and hour in which you were born. After this, you shall take care to speak the Name that is hung from your neck ten times every day while turning toward the East; you may be certain that no enchantment or any other danger shall have the power to harm you.

Furthermore, you will overcome all adversities and will be cherished and loved by the Angels and Spirits, provided that you have made their characters and that you keep them on your person. I assure you that this is the true way to succeed with ease in all your operations. By being strengthened with a Divine Name and the letters, characters, and sigils applicable to the work, you will discover with what supernatural precision and great speed both earthly and heavenly things will be obedient to you. But all of this will only be true when accompanied by the <term>Pentacles</term> <note>consecrated magical medallions or diagrams</note> which follow below, since the Seals, Characters, and Divine Names serve only to strengthen the work, to protect you from unforeseen accidents, and to attract the friendship of the Angels and Spirits. This is why, my son, before performing any experiment, I order you to read and re-read my Testament—not once, but many times—so that by being perfectly instructed in the various ceremonies, you may not fail in any way. Thus, what previously appeared difficult and long to you may, over time, become easy and very useful.

I am about to grant you many secrets, which I command you never to use for an evil purpose, for **ACCURSED IS HE WHO TAKES THE NAME OF ALMIGHTY GOD IN VAIN!** But you may use them without any other ceremonies, provided that, as I have already said, your only goal is the glory of the Eternal God. Thus, after having taught you all the ceremonies concerning the way to perform these operations, I have finally decided to make you a sharer in the secrets of which I have specific knowledge—secrets unknown to the general public until this day. However, I do this only on the condition that you do not attempt the ruin or destruction of your neighbor; his blood will cry for vengeance to God, and in the end, you and your family will feel the just wrath of an offended Deity. However, as God has not forbidden honest and lawful pleasures, you may perform the operations that follow boldly. It is always especially necessary to distinguish between good and evil, so that you may choose the former and avoid the latter. This is why I command you to be attentive to everything contained in this, my Testament.

## BOOK ONE.

### CHAPTER IX.

### CONCERNING THE RITUAL FOR STOLEN ITEMS, AND HOW IT SHOULD BE PERFORMED.

My beloved Son, if you encounter any theft, you shall do as is ordained below, and with the help of God, you shall find that which has been taken away.

If the hours and days are not otherwise specified for this operation, you must refer to what has already been said <note>referring to the astrological timing rules established in earlier chapters</note>. But before commencing any operation whatsoever for the recovery of things stolen, after having made all necessary preparations, you shall say the following prayer:

->THE PRAYER<-
<note>original: "THE ORATION"</note>

> You, O Lord God, who made the Heavens and the Earth <note>original: *Ateh Adonai Elohim Asher Ha-Shamain Ve-Ha-Aretz*</note>, etc. 
> You, O Lord, Who have made both Heaven and Earth, and have measured them in the hollow of Your hand; You Who are seated upon the Cherubim and the Seraphim, in the high places where human understanding cannot penetrate; You Who have created all things by Your agency, in Whose Presence are the Living Creatures, of which four are marvellously swift <note>original: "volatile," meaning flying or winged</note>, which have six wings, and who incessantly cry aloud: "HOLY, HOLY, HOLY, LORD GOD OF HOSTS <note>original: *QADOSCH, QADOSCH, QADOSCH, ADONAI ELOHIM TZABAOTH*</note>, Heaven and Earth are full of Your Glory;" O Lord God, You Who have expelled Adam from the Earthly Paradise, and Who have placed the Cherubim to guard the Tree of Life, You are the Lord Who alone performs wonders; show forth I pray You Your Great Mercy, by the Holy City of Jerusalem, by Your wonderful Name of four letters which are YOD, HE, VAU, HE <note>The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter biblical name of God, often represented as YHWH</note>, and by Your Holy and Admirable Name, give unto me the power and virtue to enable me to accomplish this experiment, and to come to the desired end of this operation; through You Who are Life, and unto Whom Life belongs throughout the eternal ages. Amen.

After this, perfume and incense the place by burning Temple Incense. This designated place should be pure, clean, safe from interruption or disturbance, and suitable for the work, as we shall hereafter show. Then sprinkle the designated place with consecrated Water, as is laid down in the *Chapter concerning Circles*.

The Operation being prepared in such a manner, you shall recite the invocation <note>original: "rehearse the Conjuration"</note> necessary for this experiment, at the end of which You shall say as follows:

<note>* This is simply the Hebrew of the prayer which follows; but in the manuscript codices, it is so mutilated as to be worthless.</note>

O Almighty Father and Lord, Who watches over the Heavens, the Earth, and the Abyss, mercifully grant to me by Your Holy Name written with four letters, YOD, HE, VAU, HE <note>original: "the Tetragrammaton," the four-letter Hebrew name of God</note>, that by this exorcism I may obtain power. You Who are IAH, IAH, IAH <note>original: "Jah," a shortened form of the Hebrew name for God</note>, grant that by Your power these Spirits may reveal that which we require and which we hope to find, and may they show and declare to us the persons who have committed the theft, and where they are to be found.

I conjure you, over this burning Temple Incense, once more, you Spirits named above, by all the aforementioned Names—at which all created things tremble—that you show openly to me (or to this child here present with us <note>A child was often employed as a clairvoyant in this operation; this was a common custom in the East during this period.</note>) those things which we seek.

When these things are accomplished, they will enable you to see plainly that which you seek. Take note that the Exorcist, or Master of the Art, should be such a person as is described in the Chapter concerning the Exorcist and his Companions; and if in this experiment it becomes necessary to write down symbols or Names, you shall follow the instructions regarding the pen, ink, and paper, as is duly prescribed in the chapters concerning them.

For if you do not pay attention to these things, you will neither accomplish what you desire, nor reach your intended goal.

### HOW TO KNOW WHO HAS COMMITTED A THEFT <note>This section is taken from the 1203 Lansdowne Manuscripts.</note>

Take a Sieve, after burning one-half teaspoonful of Temple Incense, and hang it by a piece of cord that has been used to hang a man; this cord should be fastened around the circumference of the rim. Within the rim, write with blood in the four sections the symbols shown in *Figure 4*. After this, take a perfectly clean brass basin which you shall fill with water from a fountain, and having pronounced these words: **DIES MIES YES-CHET BENE DONE FET DONNIMA METEMAUZ** <note>These are "barbarous names," or magical words of power, whose exact meaning is often intentionally obscure or corrupted Latin.</note>, spin the sieve with your left hand. At the same time, turn the water in the basin in the opposite direction with your right hand, stirring it with a twig of green laurel. When the water becomes still and the sieve no longer spins, gaze fixedly into the water, and you shall see the form of the person who committed the theft. In order that you may more easily recognize them, you shall mark them in some part of their face with the Magical Sword of Art; for the mark which you slash into the water with the sword shall actually be found afterward upon the person of the thief.

### THE METHOD OF MAKING THE SIEVE TURN, SO THAT YOU MAY KNOW WHO HAS COMMITTED THE THEFT <note>This is the ancient practice of coscinomancy, or divination by sieve and scissors. Because it mentions Saint Peter and Saint Paul, it was likely rewritten during the Middle Ages.</note>

Take a Sieve and stick the open points of a pair of scissors into the outside of the rim, and having rested the rings of the opened scissors...

### PRAYER

> <term>DIES MIES YES-CHET BENE DONE FET DONNIMA METEMAUZ</term> <note>These are "barbarous names of power," often used in magic. They are likely corrupted Latin or Hebrew phrases intended to sound mystical and command spiritual forces.</note>; O Lord, Who liberated the holy Susanna <note>Susanna was a biblical figure from the Book of Daniel who was falsely accused of adultery and saved from execution when the truth was revealed.</note> from a false accusation of crime; O Lord, Who liberated the holy Thekla <note>Saint Thekla was a legendary early Christian saint and follower of Paul the Apostle, said to have been miraculously saved from various executions.</note>; O Lord, Who rescued the holy Daniel from the den of lions, and the Three Children <note>Refers to Shadrach, Meshach, and Abednego, who survived being thrown into a fiery furnace in the Book of Daniel.</note> from the burning fiery furnace: free the innocent and reveal the guilty.

After this, let him or her pronounce aloud the names and surnames of all the persons living in the house where the theft has been committed, or anyone who may be suspected of having stolen the things in question, saying:

> “By Saint Peter and Saint Paul, [Name] has not done this thing.”

And let the other person reply:

> “By Saint Peter and Saint Paul, he (or she) has not done it.”

Let this be repeated three times for each person named and suspected. It is certain that upon naming the person who has committed the theft or done the crime, the sieve will turn on its own without anyone being able to stop it. By this sign, you shall know the evildoer.

# BOOK ONE.

## CHAPTER X.

### CONCERNING THE EXPERIMENT OF INVISIBILITY, AND HOW IT SHOULD BE PERFORMED.

If you wish to perform the *Experiment of Invisibility*, you shall follow these instructions. If it is necessary to observe a specific day and hour, you shall do as described in those chapters. However, if you do not need to observe the specific day and hour mentioned in that chapter, you shall follow the instructions in the chapter that precedes it. If the experiment requires you to write anything, it should be done as described in the relevant chapters, using the appropriate pen, paper, and ink—or blood <note>In historical grimoires, the use of "blood" (often from a bat or a bird) was sometimes specified for certain high-stakes magical operations to create a stronger spiritual bond.</note>. But if the matter is to be achieved through invocation, then before your conjurations, you shall devoutly say in your heart while burning *Temple Incense* <note>original: "Temple Incense" likely refers to a specific blend of aromatic resins like frankincense and myrrh used in ritual magic to "purify" the air and attract spirits.</note>:

> SCEABOLES, ARBARON, ELOHI <note>Hebrew: "God"</note>, ELIMIGITH, HERENOBULCULE, METHE, BALUTH, TIMAYAL, VILLAQUIEL, TEVENI, YEVIE, FERETE, BACUHABA, GUVARIN; through Him by Whom you have empire and power over men, you must accomplish this work so that I may go and remain invisible.

And if it is necessary in this operation to trace a Circle, you shall do as ordained in the chapter concerning Circles; and if it is necessary to write Characters <note>In this context, "Characters" refers to magical sigils or symbols believed to represent specific spirits or celestial forces.</note>, etc., you shall follow the instructions given in the respective chapters.

Once this operation is prepared, if there is a specific Conjuration to perform, you shall repeat it in the proper manner; if not, you shall say the general Conjuration, at the end of which you shall add the following words:

> O you, ALMIRAS, Master of Invisibility, with your Ministers CHEROS, MAITOR, TANGEDEM, TRANSIDIM, SUVANTOS, ABELAIOS, BORED, BELAMITH, CASTUMI, DABUEL; I conjure you by Him who makes Earth and Heaven tremble, Who is seated upon the Throne of His Majesty, that this operation may be perfectly accomplished according to my will, so that at whatever time it may please me, I may be able to be invisible.
> 
> I conjure you again, O ALMIRAS, Chief of Invisibility, both you and your Ministers, by Him through Whom all things have their being, and by SATURIEL, HARCHIEL, DANIEL, BENIEL, ASSIMONEM, that you immediately come here with all your ministers and achieve this operation as you know it ought to be accomplished; and that by the same operation, you make me invisible so that no one may be able to see me.

In order then to accomplish this aforementioned operation, you must pre-

Prepare all necessary things with required care and diligence, and put them into practice with all the general and specific ceremonies laid down for these experiments, along with all the conditions contained in our first and second Books. In these same operations, you shall also properly repeat the appropriate conjurations, with all the solemnities marked in their respective chapters. In this way, you shall accomplish the experiment surely and without hindrance, and you shall find it to be true.

But, on the contrary, if you let any of these things escape you, or if you despise them, you shall never be able to reach your proposed goal; for example, we do not easily enter a fortified city by climbing over its walls, but rather through its gates.

### HOW* TO RENDER ONESELF INVISIBLE.

Make a small image of yellow wax in the shape of a man during the month of January and on the day and hour of Saturn <note>In planetary magic, Saturn's day is Saturday. Saturn is associated with discipline, boundaries, and things that are hidden or subterranean.</note>. At that time, use a needle to write the following character (*See Figure 5*) above the crown of its head and upon its skull, which you shall have skillfully lifted. After this, put the skull back into its proper position. You shall then write the following words and characters (*See Figure 6*) on a small strip of the skin of a frog or toad which you have killed. Then, go and hang the figure by one of your own hairs from the ceiling of a cavern at the hour of midnight; while burning *Temple Incense* <note>A specific blend of aromatic resins like frankincense used to "purify" the air for ritual work.</note> under it, you shall say:

> METATRON <note>A high-ranking archangel in Jewish mystical tradition.</note>, KING <note>original: "MELEKH"</note>, BEROTH, NOTH, VENIBBETH, MACH, and all of you: I conjure you, O Figure of Wax, by the Living God, that by the power of these characters and words, you render me invisible wherever I may carry you with me. Amen <note>Hebrew: "So be it."</note>.

And after having burned *Temple Incense* under it again, bury it in the same place in a small pine wood box <note>original: "deal box"; "deal" refers to planks of fir or pine wood.</note>. Every time you wish to pass through or enter into any place without being seen, carry the figure in your left pocket and say these words:

> Come to me and never leave me, wherever I shall go.

Afterward, you shall take it carefully back to the previously mentioned place and cover it with earth until you need it again.

## CHAPTER XI.

### To Prevent a Hunter from Killing Any Game.

Take a stick of green elder wood, and hollow out the soft center <term>pith: the spongy tissue in the middle of a plant stem</term> from both ends. Inside each end, place a strip of parchment made from hare-skin, on which you have written the following symbol and word using the blood of a black hen. (*See Figure 7.*) Once you have made these two slips of parchment, place one in each end of the stick and seal the openings with more pith. Then, on a Friday in the month of February, you must purify the stick by passing it through the smoke of suitable incense three times <term>fumigate: to cleanse or consecrate an object using the smoke of burning resins or herbs</term>. Afterward, bury it in the ground beneath an elder tree <note>The elder tree has a deep history in European folklore; it was often believed to house spirits and was used in both protective and "binding" magic.</note>.

Later, you shall place it in the path where the hunter will walk. Once he has passed over it, he will have no hope of killing any game for the rest of that day. If you wish to cast the spell on him again in the same way, you only need to place the stick in his path once more. However, be sure to return it to the earth under the elder tree so that you can retrieve it whenever you need it; you must also pick the stick up each time as soon as the hunter has passed by.

\* This chapter is taken from the Lansdowne Manuscript 1203 <note>original: "1203 Lansdowne MSS." This refers to a specific 18th-century manuscript of the Key of Solomon held in the British Library.</note>.

# BOOK ONE.

## CHAPTER XII.

### HOW TO MAKE THE MAGIC GARTERS.

Take enough stag skin to make two hollow, tube-like garters,* but before sewing them up, you shall write the words and characters shown in *Figure 8* on the side of the skin that was next to the flesh. This must be written with the blood of a hare killed on the 25th of June <note>The period around June 24th, the Feast of St. John the Baptist or Midsummer, was historically considered the most potent time for gathering magical herbs and performing rituals in European folk tradition.</note>. Having filled these garters with green mugwort <term>mugwort: *Artemisia vulgaris*, a herb long associated with protection and the prevention of fatigue during travel</term> also gathered on the 25th of June before sunrise, you shall place the eye of a fish called a barbel <note>original: "barbel"; a large European freshwater fish named for the whisker-like barbels around its mouth</note> in each of the two ends of both garters. 

When you wish to use them, you shall get up before sunrise and wash them in a stream of running water, then place one on each leg above the knee. After this, you shall take a short rod made of holm-oak <note>original: "holm-oak"; the evergreen oak (*Quercus ilex*), prized in antiquity and folklore for its strength and durability</note> cut on that same 25th of June. Turn toward the direction you wish to go and write the name of your destination upon the ground. As you begin your journey, you will find it accomplished in just a few days and without any exhaustion. When you wish to stop, you only need to say **AMECH** <note>A magical word of power used here as a command to end the enchantment.</note> and strike the air with the wand mentioned before; immediately <note>original: "incontinently"; meaning instantly or without delay</note>, you will find yourself back on firm ground.

* This chapter is also taken from the Lansdowne Manuscript 1203 <note>A reference to an 18th-century manuscript of the Key of Solomon held in the British Library.</note>.

# BOOK ONE.

## CHAPTER XIII.

### HOW TO MAKE THE MAGIC CARPET SUITABLE FOR INTERROGATING THE INTELLIGENCES, SO AS TO OBTAIN AN ANSWER REGARDING WHATEVER MATTER ONE MAY WISH TO LEARN.

Make a carpet* of white and new wool. When the Moon is at her full, in the Sign of Capricorn and in the hour of the Sun, you shall go into the countryside away from any human habitation, to a place free from all impurity. There, you shall spread out your carpet so that one of its corners points toward the east and another toward the west. Having drawn a circle outside of it and enclosing it, you shall remain within, standing on the point toward the east. Holding your wand in the air for every operation, you shall call upon MICHAEL†; then, toward the north, call upon RAPHAEL; toward the west, call upon GABRIEL; and toward the south, call upon MURIEL. 

After this, you shall return to the eastern point and devoutly invoke the Great Name **AGLA** <note>AGLA is a Kabbalistic acronym for the Hebrew phrase *Atah Gibor Le-olam Adonai*, meaning "You, O Lord, are mighty forever."</note>. Take this corner of the carpet in your left hand. Turning then toward the north, you shall do the same, and continue to the other corners of the carpet, raising them so that they do not touch the ground. Holding them up in this way and turning once more toward the east, you shall say the following prayer with great reverence:

->PRAYER.<-

> **AGLA, AGLA, AGLA, AGLA**; O Almighty God, Who are the Life of the Universe and Who rule over the four divisions of its vast form by the strength and virtue of the Four Letters of Your Holy Name, the Tetragrammaton <term>Tetragrammaton: The "four-letter" name of God in Hebrew, often transliterated as YHWH or Yahweh</term>: **YOD, HE, VAU, HE**. Bless in Your Name this covering which I hold, just as You blessed the mantle of Elijah in the hands of Elisha <note>A reference to 2 Kings 2 in the Bible, where the prophet Elisha inherits the miraculous cloak of his master, Elijah.</note>. Do this so that, being covered by Your wings, nothing may be able to injure me, even as it is said: "He shall hide you under His wings, and beneath His feathers shall you trust; His truth shall be your shield and buckler."

After this, you shall fold it up, saying these following words: **RECABUSTIRA, CABUSTIRA, BUSTIRA, TIRA RA, A**; and you shall keep it carefully to serve you when needed.

When you desire to make your interrogations, choose the night of a full or new moon, between midnight and daybreak.

***

\* This Chapter is also taken from the Lansdowne Manuscript 1203 <note>Lansdowne MSS 1203 is a 17th-century manuscript of the *Key of Solomon* held in the British Library.</note>.
† I have usually found Michael attributed to the South, Raphael to the East, Gabriel to the West, and Auriel to the North. Likewise, I think the operator should turn following the course of the Sun, and not in the opposite direction as described in the text.

You shall travel to the designated spot if your purpose is to discover a treasure; if not, any place will serve, provided it is clean and pure. On the previous evening, take the precaution of writing this Character and Name (*see Figure 9*) on a strip of blue virgin parchment <term>virgin parchment: Animal skin prepared for writing that has never been used before, often sourced from a newborn or unborn animal to ensure purity.</term> using a pen made from a dove’s feather. Taking your carpet, you shall cover your head and body with it. Then, taking the censer <term>censer: A vessel used for burning incense during religious or magical rites.</term> filled with new fire <note>original: "new fire"; this traditionally refers to fire freshly struck from flint or a brand-new source, rather than a common household flame.</note>, place it in the appropriate spot and cast incense upon it. 

Then, before the incense begins to smoke, prostrate yourself on the ground with your face toward the earth. Keep the fire of the censer beneath the carpet, and hold your wand upright so that you can rest your chin against it. With your right hand, hold the strip of parchment against your forehead and say the following words:

> Vegale, Hamicata, Umsa, Terata, Yeh, Dah, Ma, Baxasoxa, Un, Horah, Himesere; O God, the Vast One, send to me the inspiration of Your Light. Allow me to discover the secret thing I ask of You, whatever that thing may be. Let me search it out with the help of Your holy ministers <note>The "ministers" named here—Raziel, Tzaphniel, and Matmoniel—are angelic beings in Kabbalistic tradition associated with the mysteries of God and the guarding of treasures.</note> Raziel, Tzaphniel, and Matmoniel. Behold, You have desired truth in the inner parts <note>original: "in the young"; this is likely a transcription error or variation of Psalm 51:6, which reads "Behold, thou desirest truth in the inward parts."</note>, and in secret things You shall make me know wisdom. Recabustira, Cabustira, Bustira, Tira, Ra, A, Karkahita, Kahita, Hita, Ta. <note>These concluding words are "Words of Power" or "Barbarous Names," which are rhythmic, phonetic formulas intended to focus the magician's intent.</note>

And you shall hear distinctly the answer which you have sought.

# BOOK ONE.

## CHAPTER XIV.

### HOW TO MASTER A TREASURE HELD BY SPIRITS.

As I have told you before, the Earth is inhabited by a great number of celestial beings and spirits <note>original: "1203 Lansdowne MSS"; this refers to one of the specific historical manuscripts in the British Library used to compile this version of the text.</note> who, through their cleverness and foresight, know the locations where treasures are hidden. It often happens that those who search for these treasures are harassed and sometimes even killed by these spirits, who are called Gnomes <term>Gnomes: In Western esotericism, these are elemental spirits of the Earth, often depicted as guardians of mines and underground riches.</term>. 

This violence is not caused by the greed of the Gnomes—for a spirit is incapable of owning anything and has no physical senses to use material goods—but because these spirits are enemies of human passions. They especially despise greed, to which humans are so prone. Foreseeing the evil ways these treasures might be used, the Gnomes have an interest in keeping the earth’s riches where they are, as they are the earth's inhabitants. When they mildly disturb those working to find such treasures, it is a warning to stop the work. If the greedy persistence of the workers forces them to continue despite these warnings, the spirits, irritated by being ignored, frequently kill the workmen. 

But know, my son, that once you are fortunate enough to become familiar with these kinds of spirits, and can command them using what I have taught you, they will be happy to share what they possess (which is useless to them), provided your goal and intention is to put the treasure to good use.

### THE MANNER OF PERFORMING THE OPERATION.

On a Sunday before sunrise, between the 10th of July and the 20th of August—when the Moon is in the sign of Leo—you shall go to the place where you have learned a treasure exists, whether through questioning the Intelligences <term>Intelligences: High-ranking spirits or angelic beings associated with planets or spheres of existence who possess hidden knowledge.</term> or by other means. There, you shall draw a circle large enough to dig within using the Sword of Magical Art <term>Sword of Magical Art: A consecrated tool in ritual magic used to command spirits and define sacred space.</term>, as the ground allows. 

Three times during the day, you shall cense the area with the incense proper for that day. Afterward, dressed in the clothing appropriate for this ritual, you shall use some kind of support or machine to hang a lamp directly over the opening. The oil for this lamp should be mixed with the fat of a man who died in the month of July <note>The use of such gruesome ingredients is common in old grimoires, often intended to create a powerful sympathetic link to the planetary or elemental forces being invoked.</note>, and the...

<summary>This page introduces the ritual for obtaining hidden treasures guarded by elemental spirits called Gnomes, emphasizing that success depends on the magician's intent and lack of greed. It details the specific astrological timing and physical preparations, including the use of a magical sword and a ritual lamp.</summary>

<keywords>Key of Solomon, treasure hunting, Gnomes, elemental spirits, Leo, ritual magic, magical tools, occult safety, planetary timing</keywords>

<page-num>52</page-num>

<header>THE KEY OF SOLOMON.</header>

...the wick being made from the cloth in which he was buried. After lighting this with fresh fire, you shall protect the workmen with a belt <note>original: "girdle"</note> made from the skin of a newly slaughtered goat. On this belt shall be written—using the blood of the same dead man from whom you took the fat—these words and characters (*see Figure 10*). You shall set them to work in safety, warning them not to be at all disturbed by the ghosts <note>original: "Spectres"</note> they will see, but to continue their work bravely. If they cannot finish the work in a single day, every time they have to leave it, you shall have them place a wooden cover over the opening, with about six inches of earth on top of the cover. You shall continue this way until the end, being present the entire time wearing your ritual robes <term>raiment of the Art: Special clothing or vestments consecrated for use in magical ceremonies.</term> and holding the Magic Sword throughout the operation. Afterward, you shall repeat this prayer:

->PRAYER.<-

> Lord <note>original: "ADONAI"</note>, God <note>original: "ELOHIM"</note>, The Mighty One <note>original: "EL"</note>, I Am That I Am <note>original: "EHEIEH ASHER EHEIEH"</note>, Prince of Princes, Existence of Existences, have mercy upon me. Cast Your eyes upon Your servant (N.) <note>"(N.)" is a placeholder where the practitioner speaks their own name.</note>, who invokes You most devoutly and prays to You by Your holy and tremendous four-letter Name <term>Tetragrammaton: The sacred four-letter Hebrew name of God, often transliterated as YHWH.</term> to be favorable. Order Your angels and spirits to come and take up their residence in this place. O you angels and spirits of the stars, O all you angels and spirits of the elements, O all you spirits who stand before the face of God: I, the minister and faithful servant of the Most High, command you—let God himself, the Existence of Existences, command you—to come and be present at this operation; I, the servant of God, most humbly entreat you. Amen.

Having then ordered the workmen to fill in the hole, you shall give the spirits permission to leave, thanking them for the favor they have shown you, and saying:

->THE LICENSE TO DEPART.<-

> O you good and happy spirits, we thank you for the benefits which we have just received from your generous bounty. Depart in peace to govern the element <note>In Western esotericism, spirits are categorized by the element they inhabit: Earth, Air, Fire, or Water.</note> which God has designated for your home. Amen.

# BOOK ONE.

## CHAPTER XV.

### Concerning the Ritual for Seeking Favor and Love.

If you wish to perform the ritual <note>original: "Experiment"</note> for seeking favor and love, observe the following instructions on how it must be carried out. If it depends on a specific day and hour, perform it during the required time as described in the chapter concerning the hours. If the ritual requires writing, you shall write as instructed in the chapter on that subject. 

If it involves binding oaths <note>original: "penal bonds"</note>, pacts, and incense <note>original: "fumigations"</note>, then you shall burn a suitable perfume as described in the chapter concerning incense. If it is necessary to sprinkle the area with water and hyssop <term>hyssop: A bushy plant often used in ancient rituals for purification and sprinkling holy water.</term>, do so according to the chapter on that subject. Similarly, if such a ritual requires symbols <note>original: "characters"</note>, names, or the like, let those names be written as described in the chapter on writing magical symbols, and place them in a clean location as previously mentioned. Then, after burning temple incense, you shall repeat the following prayer over it:

#### THE PRAYER.

> O Lord <note>original: "ADONAI"</note>, most Holy, most Righteous, and most Mighty God, Who has made all things through Your Mercy and Righteousness which fills You, grant that we may be found worthy. May this ritual be consecrated and perfect, so that the Light may issue from Your Most Holy Seat, O Lord, to obtain favor and love for us. Amen.

Once this is said, you shall wrap the object in clean silk and bury it for a day and a night at a four-way crossroads. Whenever you wish to obtain any grace or favor from anyone, take the object—having first properly consecrated it according to the rules—and hold it in your right hand. Whatever you seek shall not be denied to you. However, if you do not perform the ritual carefully and correctly, you will certainly not succeed in any way.

To obtain grace and love, write down the following words:

The sower Arepo holds the wheels with effort <note>original: "SATOR, AREPO, TENET, OPERA, ROTAS," a famous Latin word square</note>, God, God, God <note>original: "IAH, IAH, IAH"</note>, [Enam] <note>The meaning of "Enam" is obscure; it may be a corruption of a divine name or the Hebrew word for "their fountain."</note>, God, God, God <note>original: "IAH, IAH, IAH"</note>, Crown, Wisdom, Understanding, Greatness, Strength, Beauty, Victory, Splendor, Foundation, Kingdom <note>original: "KETHER, CHOKMAH, BINAH, GEDULAH, GEBURAH, TIPHERETH, NETZACH, HOD, YESOD, MALKUTH," the ten Sephiroth or attributes of God in the Kabbalah</note>, Abraham, Isaac, Jacob, Shadrach, Meshach, Abednego: be all of you present to aid me in whatever I desire to obtain.

When these words are properly written as described above, you shall find your desire brought to fulfillment.

# BOOK ONE.

## CHAPTER XVI.

### HOW OPERATIONS OF MOCKERY, INVISIBILITY, AND DECEIT SHOULD BE PREPARED.

Rituals <note>original: "Experiments"</note> relating to tricks, mockeries, and deceits may be performed in many ways.* When you wish to practice these rituals regarding any person, you shall observe the day and the hour as we have already stated. Should it be necessary to write symbols <note>original: "Characters"</note> or words, it should be done on *Virgin Parchment Paper* <term>Virgin Parchment Paper: High-quality parchment made from the skin of an animal that was newborn or had not mated, considered "pure" for ritual use.</term>, as we shall show further on. Regarding the ink, if it is not specifically prescribed for this operation, it is advisable to use the blood of a bat with the pen and the ritual needle <note>original: "needle of art"</note>. But before drawing or writing the symbols or names, all the necessary rules should be observed as they are given in the appropriate chapters; and having carefully followed all of these, you shall pronounce the following words in a loud voice:

> ABAC, ALDAL, IAT, HUDAC, GUTHAC, GUTHOR, GOMEH, WITNESS <note>original: "TISTATOR"</note>, MOCKER <note>original: "DERISOR"</note>, HE WHO ABANDONS <note>original: "DESTATUR"</note>, come here, all you who love the times and places where all kinds of mockeries and deceits are practiced. And you who make things disappear and who render them invisible, come here to deceive all those who look at these things, so that they may be misled, and that they may seem to see what they do not see and hear what they do not hear, so that their senses may be deceived and they may behold that which is not true.
> 
> Come here then and remain, and consecrate this enchantment, seeing that God the Almighty Lord has destined you for such tasks.

When this ritual is completed in this manner at the hour and time which we have shown and taught, the previously mentioned words ABAC, ALDAL, etc., should also be written with the pen as ordained hereafter; but if the ritual is performed in a different way, you shall still always say the aforementioned words, and they should be repeated as previously instructed.

If you practice these things correctly in this manner, you shall achieve the intended effect of your operations and rituals, by which you may easily deceive the senses.

---

# BOOK ONE.

## CHAPTER XVII.

### HOW EXTRAORDINARY RITUALS AND OPERATIONS SHOULD BE PREPARED.

We have spoken in the preceding chapters of common rituals <note>original: "experiments"</note> and operations, and you can easily see that we have told you enough to perform them perfectly. In this chapter, we treat extraordinary and unusual rituals, which can also be performed in many ways.

Nevertheless, those who wish to practice such rituals and operations should observe the days and hours as laid down in the proper chapters. They should also be provided with *Genuine Parchment Paper* <term>Genuine Parchment Paper: Often called "Virgin Parchment," this is writing material made from the skin of an animal that was stillborn or had not yet mated, considered ritually pure.</term> made from the skin of stillborn *Lambs*, and other necessary things. Having prepared for such a ritual, you shall say:

->PRAYER.<-

> O God, who created all things and has given us the discernment to understand good and evil; through your Holy Name, and through these Holy Names—<term>IOD</term>, <term>IAH</term>, <term>VAU</term>, <term>DALETH</term>, <term>VAU</term>, <term>TZABAOTH</term> <note>These are Hebrew letters and divine names (such as "The Lord of Hosts") frequently used in Solomonic magic.</note>, <term>ZIO</term>, <term>Lover</term> <note>original: "AMATOR"</note>, <term>Creator</term> <note>original: "CREATOR"</note>, grant, O Lord, that this ritual may become true and effective in my hands through Your Holy Seal, O <term>Lord</term> <note>original: "ADONAI"</note>, whose reign and empire remains eternal and throughout all time <note>original: "unto the Ages of the Ages"</note>. Amen.

This being done, you shall perform the ritual, observing its specific hour. You shall use perfume and incense as laid down in the proper chapter, sprinkling with exorcised water and performing all the ceremonies and formalities as we shall instruct you in the Second Book of our Key.

# BOOK ONE.

## CHAPTER XVIII.

### CONCERNING THE HOLY PENTACLES OR MEDALS.

The medals or pentacles <term>Pentacle: A magical talisman, often circular and inscribed with symbols or names of power, used to evoke spirits or protect the practitioner.</term>, which we create for the purpose of striking terror into the spirits and forcing them into obedience, also possess wonderful and excellent powers <note>original: "virtue"</note>. If you invoke the spirits by the power of these pentacles, they will obey you without resistance. When they see them, they will be struck with astonishment and fear; you will see them so overwhelmed by terror that none of them will be bold enough to oppose your will. They are also highly effective against all dangers of earth, air, water, and fire; against consumed poisons; against all kinds of illnesses and hardships; against magical binding, divination <note>original: "sortilege"</note>, and sorcery; and against all terror and fear. Wherever you find yourself, if you are armed with them, you shall be safe all the days of your life.

Through them, we acquire grace and goodwill from both men and women. Fire is extinguished, water is held back, and all creatures fear the sight of the [Divine] Names which are written upon them, obeying out of that fear.

These pentacles are usually made of the metal most suitable to the nature of the planet; in such cases, there is no need to follow the rules regarding specific colors. They should be engraved with the "Instrument of Art" <term>Instrument of Art: Consecrated tools such as the bolline or burin used for ritual engraving.</term> during the days and hours belonging to that planet.

Saturn rules over lead; Jupiter over tin; Mars over iron; the Sun over gold; Venus over copper; Mercury over mixed metals; and the Moon over silver.

They may also be made with *Genuine Parchment Paper* <note>original: "Virgin Parchment"</note>. You must write on it using the colors assigned to each planet, following the rules already established in the relevant chapters, according to which *planet* the pentacle corresponds with.

For this reason, the color black is assigned to Saturn; Jupiter rules over celestial blue; Mars over red; the Sun over gold, yellow, or lemon <note>original: "citron"</note>; Venus over green; Mercury over mixed colors; and the Moon over silver or the color of silvery earth <note>original: "argentine earth"</note>.

The material from which the *pentacle* is constructed should be *pure* <note>original: "virgin"</note>, having never been used for any other purpose; if it is made of metal, it should be purified by fire.

As for the size of the pentacles, it is up to you, as long as they are made according to the rules and with the necessary ceremonies <note>original: "solemnities"</note> that have been commanded.

The* powers <note>original: "virtues"</note> of the *Holy Pentacles* are no less beneficial to you than the secret knowledge I have already given you. You should take particular care, if you make them upon genuine parchment paper <note>original: "virgin parchment"</note>, to use the proper colors; and if you engrave them...

---

\* This and the four following paragraphs are from the Lansdowne Manuscript 1203 <note>One of the primary historical manuscripts used by S.L. MacGregor Mathers to compile this edition of the Key of Solomon.</note>.

<header>THE KEY OF SOLOMON.</header>

...upon metal, to do so in the manner taught to you; and so shall you have the satisfaction of seeing them produce the promised effect. But seeing that this science <note>original: "Science" — in this context, it refers to the systematic study of magic and spiritual laws.</note> is not a science of argument and open reasoning, but that, on the contrary, it is entirely mysterious and occult <term>Occult: Knowledge of the hidden or supernatural.</term>, we should not argue and deliberate over these matters. It is sufficient to believe firmly to enable us to bring into operation that which has already been taught.

When you construct these *Pentacles* and *Characters*, it is necessary never to forget the burning of Temple Incense, nor to employ anything beyond that which has already been taught.

It is necessary, above all things, to be attentive to the operation, and never to forget or omit those things which contribute to the success which the *Pentacles* and experiments promise. Keep ever in your mind no other intention than the Glory of God, the accomplishment of your desires, and loving-kindness towards your neighbor.

Furthermore, my beloved son, I order you not to bury this science, but to make your friends partakers in it—subject, however, to the strict command never to profane the things which are Divine. For if you do this, far from making you a friend of the spirits, it will only be the means of bringing you to destruction.

But you must never lavish these things among the ignorant, for that would be as blameworthy as to cast precious gems before swine; on the contrary, from one sage <note>original: "Sage" — a wise person or a master of this secret knowledge.</note> the secret knowledge should pass to another sage, for in this manner shall the "Treasure of Treasures" never descend into oblivion.

Adore* and revere the Most Holy Names of God which are found in these *Pentacles* and *Characters*, for without this you shall never be able to reach the end of any enterprise, nor to accomplish the *Mystery* of Mysteries.

Above all things, remember that to perform any of these operations you must be pure in body and mind, and without blemish, and omit none of the preparations.

This *Key*, full of mysteries, has been revealed to me by an angel.

Accursed be he who undertakes our art without having the qualities required to thoroughly understand our "Key"; accursed be he who invokes the Name of God in vain, for such a person prepares for himself the punishments which await the unbelievers. God shall abandon them and banish <note>original: "relegate"</note> them into the depths of Hell among the impure spirits.

For God is great and immutable <term>Immutable: Unchanging over time.</term>; He has existed forever, and He shall remain even until the end of the ages.

ACCURSED BE HE WHO TAKES THE NAME OF GOD IN VAIN! ACCURSED BE HE WHO USES THIS KNOWLEDGE FOR AN EVIL END! MAY HE BE ACCURSED IN THIS WORLD AND IN THE WORLD TO COME. AMEN. MAY HE BE ACCURSED IN THE NAME WHICH HE HAS BLASPHEMED!

<note>* The rest of the chapter is from the 1202 Lansdowne manuscripts <note>original: "1202 Lansdowne MSS" — a collection of historical documents held in the British Library.</note>, except for the last sentence.</note>

<header>THE KEY OF SOLOMON.</header>

->THE FOLLOWING ARE THE HOLY PENTACLES, PRESENTED IN THEIR PROPER FIGURES AND CHARACTERS, TOGETHER WITH THEIR SPECIFIC POWERS; FOR THE USE OF THE MASTER OF THE <term>ART</term>. <note>The "Art" refers to the practice of ceremonial magic.</note><-

## THE ORDER OF THE PENTACLES.

->(1) Seven <term>Pentacles</term> <note>Magical talismans or symbols, usually circular, inscribed with names of power</note> consecrated to Saturn = Black.<-
->(2) Seven Pentacles consecrated to Jupiter = Blue.<-
->(3) Seven Pentacles consecrated to Mars = Red.<-
->(4) Seven Pentacles consecrated to the Sun = Yellow.<-
->(5) Five Pentacles consecrated to Venus = Green.<-
->(6) Five Pentacles consecrated to Mercury = Mixed Colors.<-
->(7) Six Pentacles consecrated to the Moon = Silver.<-

*Editor's Note on Figure 1.*—The Mystical Figure of SOLOMON.—This is only found in two manuscripts, Lansdowne 1202 and 1203. It was featured by Eliphas Lévi in his *Dogma and Ritual of High Magic* <note>original: "Dogme et Rituel de la Haute Magie"</note>, and by Tycho Brahé in his *Natural Magical Calendar* <note>original: "Calendarium Naturale Magicum"</note>, but in each instance without the Hebrew words and letters, probably because these were so distorted by uneducated copyists as to be unrecognizable. After much labor and study of the figure, I believe the words in the body of the symbol are intended to be the *Ten Sephiroth* <note>The ten attributes/emanations in the Kabbalah through which the Infinite reveals itself</note> arranged in the form of the *Tree of Life*, with the name of SOLOMON to the right and to the left; while the surrounding characters are intended to be the twenty-two letters of the *Hebrew Alphabet*. I have, therefore, restored them accordingly. This figure serves as the frontispiece in each of the mentioned manuscripts.

---

### SATURN.

Figure 11.—The First Pentacle of Saturn.—This *Pentacle* is of great value and utility for striking terror into spirits. Therefore, when it is shown to them, they submit, and kneeling upon the earth before it, they obey.

*Editor's Note.*—The Hebrew letters within the square are the four great Names of God which are written with four letters:—IHVH <note>The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter name of God</note>, Yod,

<summary>This page introduces the planetary pentacles of Solomon, listing their governing planets and required colors, while providing editorial context on the mystical "Figure of Solomon" and the first pentacle of Saturn.</summary>
<keywords>Pentacles, Solomon, Saturn, Jupiter, Mars, Sun, Venus, Mercury, Moon, Sephiroth, Tree of Life, Hebrew Alphabet, Tetragrammaton, talismans</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the catalog of the Pentacles of Saturn, detailing the spiritual significance and numerical calculations behind the Second, Third, and Fourth Pentacles.</meta>

<header>THE KEY OF SOLOMON.</header>

He, Vau, He <note>The letters of the Tetragrammaton, the sacred four-letter name of God</note>; ADNI, Adonai <note>Hebrew: "My Lord"</note>; IIAI, Yiai (this name has the same numerical value in Hebrew as the name EL <note>Hebrew: "God"</note>); and AHIH, Eheieh <note>Hebrew: "I Will Be"</note>. The Hebrew verse which surrounds it is from *Psalm 72:9*:

> "The Ethiopians shall kneel before him; his enemies shall lick the dust."

---

**Figure 12.**—The Second Pentacle of Saturn.—This *pentacle* <note>A magical talisman or symbol, usually circular, inscribed with names of power</note> is of great value against adversaries, and it is especially useful for subduing the pride of the spirits.

*Editor's Note.*—This is the celebrated

->SATOR<-
->AREPO<-
->TENET<-
->OPERA<-
->ROTAS,<-
<note>original Latin: "SATOR AREPO TENET OPERA ROTAS." This is a famous ancient palindrome and acrostic. While its exact translation is debated, it is often rendered as "The sower Arepo holds the wheels with effort."</note>

the most perfect existing form of a double acrostic in terms of the arrangement of the letters. It is repeatedly mentioned in the records of medieval magic, but its derivation from this specific *pentacle* has been unknown to all but a very few. It is immediately obvious that it is a square of five, containing twenty-five letters; when these are added to the "unity" (the number one), the total is twenty-six, which is the numerical value of IHVH <note>The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter name of God (Yod-He-Vav-He)</note>. The Hebrew verse surrounding it is taken from *Psalm 72:8*:

> "His dominion shall be also from one sea to the other, and from the flood unto the world's end."

This passage also consists of exactly twenty-five letters in Hebrew. Its total numerical value (counting the final forms of Hebrew letters with their increased values), when added to the value of the name *Elohim* <note>A Hebrew name for God representing divine justice or power</note>, is exactly equal to the total numerical value of the twenty-five letters in the Square.

---

**Figure 13.**—The Third Pentacle of Saturn.—This should be made within the *Magical Circle*, and it is good for use at night when you invoke the spirits that share the nature of Saturn.

*Editor's Note.*—The characters at the ends of the rays of the Mystic Wheel are magical symbols of Saturn. Surrounding it are the names of the angels written in Hebrew: Omeliel, Anachiel, Arauchiah, and Anazachia.

---

**Figure 14.**—The Fourth Pentacle of Saturn.—This pentacle serves principally for carrying out all experiments and operations of ruin, destruction, and death.

## Figure 15.—The Fifth Pentacle of Saturn.

This *Pentacle* defends those who invoke the Spirits of Saturn during the night; and it chases away the Spirits which guard treasures.

Around the four sides of the Square are the Names of the Angels:—Arehanah, Rakhaniel, Roelhaiphar, and Noaphiel. The verse is:—"A Great God, a Mighty, and a Terrible."—*Deuteronomy 10:17.*

Around this *Pentacle* is each Name symbolized as it should be. The person against whom you shall pronounce it shall be obsessed by Demons.

*Editor’s Note.*—It is formed from Mystical Characters of Saturn. Around it is written in Hebrew: "Set a wicked one to be ruler over him, and let Satan stand at his right hand." <note>This is a quote from Psalm 109:6</note>

## Figure 17.—The Seventh and Last Pentacle of Saturn.

This *Pentacle* is fit for exciting earthquakes, seeing that the power of each order of Angels invoked here is sufficient to make the whole Universe tremble.

7. **ELOHIM**, Gods; 
8. **BENI ELOHIM**, Sons of the Elohim;

## JUPITER.

Figure 18.—The First <term>Pentacle</term> <note>A magical talisman or symbol, usually circular, inscribed with names of power</note> of Jupiter.—This serves to invoke the Spirits of Jupiter, and especially those whose names are written around the <term>Pentacle</term>. Among them, <term>Parasiel</term> is the Lord and Master of Treasures, and he teaches how to become the possessor of the places where they are hidden.

*Editor's Note.*—This <term>Pentacle</term> is composed of the mystical characters of Jupiter. Around it are the names of the angels:—<term>Netoniel</term>, <term>Devachiah</term>, <term>Tzedeqiah</term>, and <term>Parasiel</term>, written in Hebrew.

Figure 19.—The Second <term>Pentacle</term> of Jupiter.—This is suitable for acquiring glory, honors, high status, riches, and all kinds of prosperity, along with great peace of mind. It is also used to discover treasures and to drive away the spirits that guard them. It should be written upon *virgin parchment* <note>Parchment made from the skin of an animal that has not mated, used to ensure ritual purity</note> with a swallow's quill and the blood of a screech owl.

*Editor's Note.*—In the center of the <term>Hexagram</term> <note>A six-pointed star formed by two overlapping triangles</note> are the letters of the Name <term>AHIH</term>, <term>Eheieh</term> <note>original Hebrew: "I AM" or "I Will Be," a name of God from Exodus 3:14</note>. In the upper and lower angles of the hexagram are those of the Name <term>AB</term>, meaning "the Father." In the remaining angles are the letters of the Name <term>IHVH</term> <note>The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter name of God, often rendered as Yahweh</note>. I believe the letters outside the <term>Hexagram</term> in the inner angles are intended for those of the first two words of the verse, which is taken from *Psalm 112:3*:—
> "*Wealth and riches are in his house, and his righteousness endures forever.*"

Figure 20.—The Third <term>Pentacle</term> of Jupiter.—This defends and protects those who invoke and summon the spirits. When they appear, show them this <term>Pentacle</term> and they will obey immediately.

*Editor's Note.*—In the upper left-hand corner is the *Magical Seal of Jupiter* with the letters of the Name <term>IHVH</term>. In the others are the Seal of the Intelligence of Jupiter and the Names <term>Adonai</term> <note>original Hebrew: "Lord"</note> and <term>IHVH</term>. Around it is the verse from *Psalm 125:1*:—
> "*A song of degrees. They who trust in IHVH shall be like Mount Zion, which cannot be moved, but remains forever.*"

# THE KEY OF SOLOMON

**Figure 21.—The Fourth Pentacle of Jupiter.**—It serves to acquire riches and honor, and to possess much wealth. Its Angel is Bariel. It should be engraved upon silver in the day and hour of Jupiter when the planet is in the zodiac sign of Cancer.

*Editor's Note.*—Above the magical sigil <note>original: Sigil; a symbolic seal or sign used in magic</note> is the Name IH, Iah <note>a shortened form of the Hebrew name for God, Yahweh</note>. Below it are the names of the Angels Adoniel and Bariel; the letters of the latter are arranged around a square of four compartments. Around it is the verse <note>original: Versicle</note> from *Psalm 112:3*:—"*Wealth and riches are in his house, and his righteousness endures forever.*"

---

**Figure 22.—The Fifth Pentacle of Jupiter.**—This has great power. It serves to provide guaranteed visions. Jacob, being armed with this *Pentacle*, beheld the ladder which reached up to heaven.

*Editor's Note.*—The Hebrew letters within the *Pentacle* are taken from the last five words of the verse which surrounds it, each of which contains five letters. These are then recombined to form certain mystical names. The verse is taken from *Ezekiel 1:1*:—"*As I was among the captives by the river of Chebar, the heavens were opened, and I saw visions of God <note>original: Elohim</note>.*" In my opinion, the verse should only consist of the last five words; if so, the historical contradiction <note>original: anachronism</note> of Jacob using a *Pentacle* containing a sentence from the much later Book of Ezekiel would no longer exist.

---

**Figure 23.—The Sixth Pentacle of Jupiter.**—It serves for protection against all earthly dangers by gazing at it devoutly each day and repeating the verse which surrounds it: "*Thus you shall never perish.*"

*Editor's Note.*—The four names in the arms of the cross are: Seraph, Kerub, Ariel, and Tharsis—the four rulers of the elements <note>Fire, Earth, Air, and Water</note>. The verse is from *Psalm 22:16–17*:—"*They pierced my hands and my feet; I may count all my bones.*"

---

**Figure 24.—The Seventh and last Pentacle of Jupiter.**—It has great power against poverty if you contemplate it with devotion while repeating the verse. Furthermore, it serves to drive away those spirits who guard treasures and helps to discover the same.

*Editor's Note.*—Mystical characters of Jupiter with the verse:

### MARS

**Figure 25.—The First Pentacle of Mars.**—It is suitable for invoking spirits belonging to the nature of Mars, especially those whose names are written within the Pentacle.

*Editor’s Note.*—The mystical characters of Mars and the names of the four Angels—Madimiel, Bartzachiah, Eschiel, and Ithuriel—are written in Hebrew around the Pentacle.

**Figure 26.—The Second Pentacle of Mars.**—This Pentacle is used with great success against all kinds of diseases if it is applied to the afflicted part of the body.

*Editor’s Note.*—The Hebrew letter Hé <note>the fifth letter of the Hebrew alphabet, often symbolizing the divine breath or life</note> is placed in the corners of the hexagram <note>a six-pointed star</note>. Within the star are the names IHVH <note>the Tetragrammaton, the four-letter name of God</note>, IHShVH Yeheshuah <note>a mystical Hebrew name for Jesus or Joshua, created by inserting the letter 'Shin,' representing the Holy Spirit, into the center of the name of God</note>, and Elohim <note>a Hebrew name for God meaning 'Divine Powers'</note>. Surrounding it is the sentence from *John 1:4*:—“In Him was life, and the life was the light of mankind.” This may be presented as an argument for the great antiquity of the first few mystical verses of the Gospel of Saint John.

**Figure 27.—The Third Pentacle of Mars.**—It is of great value for inciting war, wrath, discord, and hostility. It is also used for resisting enemies and striking terror into rebellious spirits. The names of the All-Powerful God are explicitly marked within it.

*Editor’s Note.*—The letters of the names Eloah <note>a singular form of the Hebrew word for God</note> and Shaddaï <note>a Hebrew name for God meaning 'The Almighty'</note> are present. In the center is the large letter Vau <note>the sixth letter of the Hebrew alphabet</note>, which is the signature <note>symbolic sign</note> of the Qabalistic Microprosopus <note>original: Microprosopus; a mystical term for the 'Lesser Countenance' or the manifest aspect of God in the world</note>. Around it is the verse <note>original: versicle</note> from *Psalm 77:13*:—“Who is so great a God as our Elohim?”

**Figure 28.—The Fourth Pentacle of Mars.**—It possesses great virtue and power in war; therefore, it will undoubtedly grant you victory.

# THE KEY OF SOLOMON.

*Editor’s Note.*—In the center is the great name **Agla** <note>original: Agla, a Kabbalistic acronym for the Hebrew phrase "Atah Gibor Le-olam Adonai," meaning "You, O Lord, are mighty forever"</note>; to the right and left are the letters of the name **IHVH** <note>the Tetragrammaton, the four-letter name of God</note>; above and below is **El** <note>a Hebrew name for God</note>. Surrounding it is the verse from Psalm 110:5:—“*The Lord at your right hand shall shatter kings on the day of His wrath.*”

***

### Figure 29.—The Fifth Pentacle of Mars.
Write this *Pentacle* upon *virgin parchment* <term>parchment made from the skin of an animal that has not reached maturity, traditionally required for ritual purity</term>, because it is terrifying to demons. At its sight and appearance, they will obey you, for they cannot resist its presence.

*Editor’s Note.*—Around the figure of the Scorpion is the word **HVL**. The verse is from Psalm 91:13:—“*You shall tread upon the lion and the cobra; you shall trample the young lion and the serpent under your feet.*”

***

### Figure 30.—The Sixth Pentacle of Mars.
It has such great power that if you are armed with it and attacked by anyone, you will be neither injured nor wounded when you fight them, and their own weapons shall turn against them.

*Editor’s Note.*—Around the eight points of the *Pentacle's* rays are the words “**Elohim qeber**,” which means “God has covered” or “God has protected” <note>original: Elohim qeber</note>, written in the Secret Alphabet of Malachim <note>also known as the "Writing of the Angels," an occult script used by Renaissance magicians</note>. The verse is from Psalm 37:15:—“*Their sword shall enter into their own heart, and their bows shall be broken.*”

***

### Figure 31.—The Seventh and Last Pentacle of Mars.
Write this upon *virgin parchment* with the blood of a bat, during the day and hour of Mars <note>referring to the system of "planetary hours" where specific times of day are ruled by specific planets</note>. Uncover it within the magic circle while invoking the demons whose names are written inside it, and you will immediately see hail and storms.

*Editor’s Note.*—In the center of the Pentacle are the divine names, **El** and **Yiai**, which have the same numerical value <term>gematria; a system where Hebrew letters represent numbers, and words with the same sum are considered spiritually connected</term> when written in Hebrew. The letters in Hebrew and in the secret "Celestial Alphabet" form the names of spirits. Around the Pentacle is:—“*He gave them hail for rain, and flaming fire in their land. He struck their vines also, and their fig trees.*”—Psalm 105:32–33.

<summary>This page describes the final four Pentacles of Mars, detailing their powers to command demons, provide invulnerability in combat, and summon elemental storms.</summary>
<keywords>Pentacles of Mars, Solomon, Agla, IHVH, protection, Malachim alphabet, Celestial alphabet, ritual magic, virgin parchment, spiritual warfare</keywords>

# THE KEY OF SOLOMON.

## THE SUN.

### Figure 32.—The First <term>Pentacle</term> of the Sun.
The Face of Shaddai <note>a Hebrew name for God, usually translated as "The Almighty"</note> the Almighty, at whose appearance all creatures obey, and the angelic spirits do reverence on bended knees.

*Editor’s Note.*—This unique <term>Pentacle</term> contains the head of the great Angel Metatron <note>also spelled Methraton</note>, the deputy and representative of Shaddai. He is called the "Prince of Countenances" and is the right-hand masculine Cherub of the Ark of the Covenant, just as Sandalphon is the left-hand feminine Cherub. On either side is the name “El Shaddai.” Written around the edge in English is: “*Behold His face and form by whom all things were made, and whom all creatures obey*” <note>original Latin: *Ecce faciem et figuram ejus per quem omnia facta sunt, et cui omnia serviunt creaturae*</note>.

***

### Figure 33.—The Second <term>Pentacle</term> of the Sun.
This <term>Pentacle</term>, along with the one before it and the one following, belongs to the nature of the Sun. They serve to restrain the pride and arrogance of the Solar Spirits, who are naturally very proud and arrogant.

*Editor’s Note.*—Contains mystical characters of the Sun and the names of the Angels: Shemeshiel, Paimoniah, Rekhodiah, and Malkhiel.

***

### Figure 34.—The Third <term>Pentacle</term> of the Sun.
In addition to the effects of the previous two, this serves to help one acquire a kingdom and empire, to inflict loss on enemies, and to gain fame and glory—especially through the name of God, the <term>Tetragrammaton</term> <note>the sacred four-letter name of God, often rendered as YHWH or Yahweh</note>, which is written within it twelve times.

*Editor’s Note.*—The name **IHVH** is repeated twelve times. There is also a verse similar to *Daniel 4:34*:
> “*My Kingdom is an everlasting Kingdom, and my dominion endures from age to age.*”

***

### Figure 35.—The Fourth <term>Pentacle</term> of the Sun.
This serves to enable you to see Spirits when they appear invisible to those who invoke them. Once you have uncovered this pentacle, they will immediately become visible.

*Editor’s Note.*—The names **IHVH** and **Adonai** <note>a Hebrew title for God meaning "Lord"</note> are written in the center in Hebrew. Around the rays are characters from the mystical alphabet known as the "Passing of the River" <note>original: *Transitus Fluvii*, an occult script based on the Hebrew alphabet</note>. The verse is from *Psalm 13:3–4*:
> “*Lighten my eyes so that I do not sleep in death, lest my enemy say, 'I have prevailed against him.'*”

<summary>This page details the first four Pentacles of the Sun, which are used to invoke the authority of God (Shaddai) and the angel Metatron to command spirits and gain worldly power or spiritual sight.</summary>
<keywords>The Sun, Pentacle, Shaddai, Metatron, Solar Spirits, Tetragrammaton, IHVH, Adonai, Passing of the River alphabet, King Solomon</keywords>

# THE KEY OF SOLOMON

Figure 36.—The Fifth Pentacle of the Sun.—It serves to invoke those spirits who can transport you from one place to another, over a long distance and in a short time.

*Editor's Note.*—Characters in the “*Passing of the River*” <note>original: *Transitus Fluvii*, an occult alphabet derived from Hebrew</note> alphabet, forming spirits' names. The verse is from *Psalm 91:11–12*:—“*He shall give His angels charge over you, to keep you in all your ways. They shall bear you up in their hands.*”

***

Figure 37.—The Sixth Pentacle of the Sun.—When correctly made, it works excellently for the operation of invisibility.

*Editor's Note.*—In the center is the mystical letter *Yod* <note>The tenth letter of the Hebrew alphabet, often symbolizing the spark of creation or the hand of God</note> in the Celestial Alphabet. The three letters in the “*Passing of the River*” script in the corners of the triangle form the great name *Shaddaï* <note>meaning "The Almighty"</note>. The words in the same characters around its three sides are, in my opinion, from *Genesis 1:1*:—“*In the beginning God <note>original: Elohim</note> created, etc.*”; but the characters are badly damaged in the manuscripts. The verse is from *Psalms 69:23 and 135:16*:—“*Let their eyes be darkened so they cannot see; and make their loins shake continually. They have eyes, but they do not see.*”

***

Figure 38.—The Seventh and Last Pentacle of the Sun.—If anyone happens to be imprisoned or held in iron chains, the presence of this pentacle—which should be engraved on gold during the day and hour of the Sun—will cause them to be immediately delivered and set free.

*Editor's Note.*—On the arms of the cross are written the names of Chasan, Angel of Air; Arel, Angel of Fire; Phorlakh, Angel of Earth; and Taliahad, Angel of Water. Between the four arms of the cross are written the names of the four rulers of the elements: Ariel, Seraph, Tharshis, and Cherub. The verse is from *Psalm 116:16–17*:—“*You have broken my bonds apart. I will offer to You the sacrifice of thanksgiving and will call upon the Name of the LORD <note>original: IHVH, the Tetragrammaton or four-letter name of God</note>.*”

***

*Editor’s Note.*—Mystical Characters of Venus, and the Names of the Angels Nogahiel, Acheliah, Socodiah (or Socohiah), and Nangariel.

*Figure 40.*—The Second <term>Pentacle</term> <note>A talisman or magical diagram, usually inscribed within a circle, used for protection or to command spirits</note> of Venus.—These pentacles are also suitable for obtaining grace and honor, and for all things which belong to Venus, and for accomplishing all your desires in this matter.

*Figure 41.*—The Third Pentacle of Venus.—If this is only shown to any person, it serves to attract love. Its angel, Monachiel, should be invoked on the day and hour of Venus, at one o’clock or at eight.

*Editor’s Note.*—The following names are written within the figure: IHVH <note>The Tetragrammaton, the sacred four-letter name of God</note>, Adonai <note>Lord</note>, Ruach <note>Spirit</note>, Achides, Ægalmiel, Monachiel, and Degaliel. The verse is from *Genesis 1:28*:—
> “*And the Elohim <note>God</note> blessed them, and the Elohim said to them, 'Be fruitful and multiply, and fill the earth and subdue it.'*”

***

*Figure 42.*—The Fourth Pentacle of Venus.—It is of great power, since it compels the spirits of Venus to obey, and can instantly force any person you wish to come to you.

*Editor’s Note.*—At the four angles of the figure are the four letters of the name IHVH. The other letters form the names of the spirits of Venus, for example: Schii, Eli, Ayib, etc. The verse is from *Genesis 2:23, 24*:—
> “*This is bone of my bones, and flesh of my flesh. And they two were one flesh.*”

*Figure 43.*—The Fifth and Last Pentacle of Venus.—When it is only shown to any person whatsoever, it wonderfully incites and excites them toward love.

*Editor’s Note.*—Around the central square are the names Elohim <note>God</note>, El Gebil <note>God of the Heights or the Great God</note>, and two other names which I cannot decipher and have, therefore, given them as they stand. The characters are those of the “*Passing of the River*” <note>original: *Transitus Fluvii*, an occult alphabet derived from Hebrew</note>. The surrounding verse is from *Psalm 22:14*:—

### MERCURY.

**Figure 44.—The First Pentacle of Mercury.**—It is used to invoke the spirits who dwell beneath the firmament <note>original: "serveth to invoke the Spirits who are under the Firmament"; the firmament refers to the sky or heavens</note>.

*Editor’s Note.*—Letters forming the names of the spirits *Yekahel* and *Agiel*.

---

**Figure 45.—The Second Pentacle of Mercury.**—The spirits written here serve to bring about and grant things that are contrary to the laws of nature and which are not categorized under any other heading. They give answers easily, but they are difficult to see.

*Editor’s Note.*—The letters form the names of Böel and other spirits.

---

**Figure 46.—The Third Pentacle of Mercury.**—This and the following pentacle serve to invoke the spirits subject to Mercury, especially those whose names are written in this *pentacle*.

*Editor’s Note.*—Mystical characters of Mercury and the names of the angels: Kokaviel, Gheoriah, Savaniah, and Chokmahiel.

---

**Figure 47.—The Fourth Pentacle of Mercury.**—This is further used to acquire the understanding and knowledge of all created things, to seek out and penetrate hidden secrets, and to command those spirits called Allatori <note>Allatori: likely derived from the Latin "alatus," meaning winged; these spirits act as messengers or emissaries</note> to act as ambassadors. They obey very readily.

---

**Figure 48.—The Fifth and Last Pentacle of Mercury.**—This commands the spirits of Mercury and is used to open doors in whatever way they may be closed; nothing it encounters can resist it.

->THE KEY OF SOLOMON<-

## PREFATORY NOTE TO BOOK TWO.

This work of Solomon is divided into two books. In the first, you may see and learn how to avoid errors in experiments, operations, and regarding the spirits themselves. In the second, you are taught how the magical arts may be applied to their intended purpose and goal.

<note>This Prefatory Note is only found in Harleian Manuscript 3981, Sloane Manuscript 3091, and King’s Manuscript 288.</note>

For this reason, you should take great care and caution that this Key of Secrets <note>original: "Clavicula," the Latin title for the work</note> does not fall into the hands of the foolish, the stupid, or the ignorant. For whoever possesses this and uses it according to the instructions contained here will not only be able to direct the magical arts toward their intended goal but will also be able to correct any errors he might find within this text.

No art or operation of this kind will reach its conclusion unless the Master of the Art, or Exorcist, has this work completely in his power—meaning he must thoroughly understand it. Without this understanding, he will never achieve the desired effect of any operation.

For this reason, I earnestly pray and command <term>original: "conjure"; in this context, to solemnly charge or appeal to someone</term> the person who receives this Key of Secrets neither to share it nor to allow anyone to participate in this knowledge unless they are faithful, capable of keeping a secret, and skilled in these arts. And I most humbly beg the owner of this book—by the unutterable four-letter name of God, Yod, He, Vav, He <note>the Tetragrammaton, or YHWH</note>, and by the name Adonai <note>Hebrew for "Lord"</note>, and by all the other most high and holy names of God—that he value this work as dearly as his own soul and ensure that no foolish or ignorant person shares in it.

***

<summary>This preface introduces Book Two of the Key of Solomon, warning that the text must be kept secret and only used by those who have mastered its instructions. The author invokes the holy names of God to emphasize the gravity of protecting this knowledge from the ignorant.</summary>
<keywords>Solomon, Key of Secrets, Magical Arts, Exorcist, Tetragrammaton, YHWH, Adonai, secrecy, occult study</keywords>
